</p> <p>Scaleway is now listed on the <a href="https://marketplace.upbound.io/providers/scaleway/provider-scaleway/v0.1.0">official Upbound repository</a></p> <p>Refer to the <a href="https://github.com/scaleway/crossplane-provider-scaleway">Scaleway Crossplaine Provider repository</a> for more information.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#initial-support-for-crossplane https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#initial-support-for-crossplane compute <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Initial support for kOps]]> <p>We added initial support for <a
href="https://github.com/kubernetes/kops/blob/master/docs/install.md">kOps</a>.
Refer to the <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/tutorials/getting-started-with-kops-on-scaleway/">Getting started with kOps on Scaleway tutorial page</a> for more information.
</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#initial-support-for-kops https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#initial-support-for-kops containers <![CDATA[Load-balancers | HTTP redirect and unconditional ACLs are now available!]]> <p>Load Balancer now lets you redirect client requests to alternative URLs.
You can use this feature to secure your service by redirecting all HTTP traffic to HTTPS, or to redirect users to a backup service when carrying out maintenance.</p> <p>HTTP redirects come as a new type of ACL configured within a frontend.
You can fully customize the redirection URL, from the protocol scheme all the way to the query parameters, using powerful templated placeholders to reuse parts of the original requests.</p> <p>Furthermores, we are introducing the ability to create unconditioned ACLs, allowing you to explicitly define a default and catch all behavior for your frontend’s ACL.</p> <p>Check out our documentation to find out <a href="/load-balancer/how-to/create-manage-acls/">how to create and manage your ACLs</a> to get started, or our <a href="/load-balancer/reference-content/acls/">reference documentation</a>
for a deeper dive into ACLs.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#http-redirect-and-unconditional-acls-are-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#http-redirect-and-unconditional-acls-are-now-available network <![CDATA[Terraform | Terraform Scaleway provider v2.16.3 is available!]]> <p>Several new features for the Domains and DNS and Kubernetes products have been added.
A variety of minor issues has been fixed.
Refer to the <a href="https://github.com/scaleway/terraform-provider-scaleway/releases/tag/v2.16.3">official release notes</a> for more information.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#terraform-scaleway-provider-v2163-is-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#terraform-scaleway-provider-v2163-is-available devtools <![CDATA[Instances | New OS images are available]]> <p>We released the Fedora 38 OS image on the official release day!</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-os-images-are-available
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-os-images-are-available compute <![CDATA[Cockpit | Cockpit limitations]]> <p>Limitations for our Mimir metrics ingestor is now available!
</p> <p>Refer to <a href="/cockpit/reference-content/cockpit-limitations/#limits-for-mimir">our Mimir metrics documentation</a> to find out about the limitations for Mimir.</p> <p>If you need custom limitations, feel free to <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/fr/betas/#observability">reach out to us</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cockpit-limitations https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cockpit-limitations observability <![CDATA[Functions | It is now easier to deploy Python API]]> <p>We have created a Python framework that allows you to easily create and deploy an
API on Serverless Functions using Python annotations.
You can even add an API Gateway hosted on Serverless Containers to manage routing from a single base URL.&nbsp;</p> <p>To deploy an example application with the self-hosted API gateway, refer to our official <a href="https://github.com/scaleway/serverless-examples/tree/main/functions/serverless-gateway-python#using-serverless-gateway-and-serverless-apis">GitHub examples repository</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#it-is-now-easier-to-deploy-python-api https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#it-is-now-easier-to-deploy-python-api serverless <![CDATA[Functions | Serverless
offline testing for Node is available!]]> <p>Developing functions is now much easier with our local development framework for <a href="https://github.com/scaleway/serverless-functions-node#%EF%B8%8F-quickstart">Node JS</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-offline-testing-for-node-is-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-offline-testing-for-node-is-available serverless <![CDATA[Functions | Serverless Go runtimes updates!]]> <p>Go 1.20 runtime beta ended and it is now available!
Go 1.17 runtime is no longer maintained by Golang team and is now deprecated.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-go-runtimes-updates https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-go-runtimes-updates serverless <![CDATA[Object-storage | IAM at the resource level is now in Beta!]]> <p>It is now possible to designate users or applications as principals of bucket policies, providing access management granularity at the resource level on top of IAM.</p> <p>Check out our documentation to start <a
href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/object-storage/api-cli/combining-iam-and-object-storage">setting up granular access to Object Storage</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#iam-at-the-resource-level-is-now-in-beta https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#iam-at-the-resource-level-is-now-in-beta storage <![CDATA[Functions | NodeJS 20 is available in beta]]> <p>A new runtime has been added:</p> <ul> <li>Node20 in beta.
It includes the latest version of nodeJs as well as all the improvements that were added to node19.</li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#nodejs-20-is-available-in-beta https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#nodejs-20-is-available-in-beta serverless <![CDATA[Functions | Node19 runtime is deprecated]]> <p>Node19 is now deprecated as it is no longer maintained by the nodeJs team.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#node19-runtime-is-deprecated https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#node19-runtime-is-deprecated serverless <![CDATA[Transactional-email |

</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#addition-of-an-smtp-host-and-ports-in-terraform https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#addition-of-an-smtp-host-and-ports-in-terraform managed-services <![CDATA[Transactional-email | A management policy for improving domain reputation is available!]]> <p>Find out <a href="/transactional-email/reference-content/protecting-your-domain-reputation/">how we protect the reputation of your domain</a> with progressive emailing!
</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#a-management-policy-for-improving-domain-reputation-is-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#a-management-policy-for-improving-domain-reputation-is-available managed-services <![CDATA[Transactional-email | New email alerts from Transactional Email for account activities]]> <p>Transactional Email now sends alerts for quotas, abnormal activity, and domain addition.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-email-alerts-from-transactional-email-for-account-activities

<li><code>TransactionalEmailEmailReadyOnly</code></li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-iam-permission-set-for-transactional-email https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-iam-permission-set-for-transactional-email managed-services <![CDATA[Transactional-email | Send emails via Terraform using Transactional Email and Serverless!]]> <p>Find out <a href="/tutorials/send-emails-with-serverless-and-tem/">how to send emails via Terraform using Transactional Email and Serverless</a>.</p>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#send-emails-via-terraform-using-transactional-email-and-serverless https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#send-emails-via-terraform-using-transactional-email-and-serverless managed-services <![CDATA[Secret-manager | Secret Manager is now in General Availability]]> <p>Secret Manager is now in General Availability.</p> <p>For more information, refer to the <a href="/secret-manager/quickstart/">Secret Manager product documentation</a> or the <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/secret-manager/">Secret Manager product website</a></p>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#secret-manager-is-now-in-general-availability https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#secret-manager-is-now-in-general-availability security-identity <![CDATA[Block-storage | Block Storage Low Latency is available with Cockpit]]> <p>Cockpit now supports Block Storage Low Latency with a managed dashboard in Grafana.
Your can now track your Block Storage Low Latency consumption for each AZ, in order to improve the management of the capacity you consume.
We are working on displaying performance information.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#block-storage-low-latency-is-available-with-cockpit https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#block-storage-low-latency-is-available-with-cockpit storage <![CDATA[Cockpit | Introducing volumes discounts for your custom metrics!]]> <p>Check out our latest update: volume discounts for your custom metrics, allowing you to enjoy a cost-efficient monitoring solution!</p> <p><strong>How it works:</strong> The monthly pricing for custom metrics will be calculated in levels.
<strong>Scaleway applies six levels of volume discounts.</strong></p> <table> <thead> <tr> <th>Levels</th> <th>Range</th> <th>Monthly pricing with volume discounts</th> </tr> </thead> <tbody><tr> <td>Level one</td> <td>From 0 to 10 billion custom metrics</td> <td>€0.15 per million samples</td> </tr> <tr> <td>Level two</td> <td>From 10 to 25 billion custom metrics</td> <td>€0.13 per million samples</td> </tr> <tr> <td>Level three</td> <td>From 25 to 50 billion custom metrics</td> <td>€0.12 per million samples</td> </tr> <tr> <td>Level four</td> <td>From 50 to 100 billion custom metrics</td>
<td>€0.11 per million samples</td> </tr> <tr> <td>Level five</td> <td>From 100 to 200 billion custom metrics</td> <td>€0.10 per million samples</td> </tr> <tr> <td>Level six</td> <td>From 200 billion custom metrics and beyond</td> <td>€0.08 per million samples</td> </tr> </tbody></table> <p>Here is an example of how you would be billed for <strong>sending 52 billion custom metrics samples per month</strong>:</p> <ul> <li>10 billion samples at €0.15 per million samples: €1.500</li> <li>15 billion samples at €0.13 per million samples: €1.950</li> <li>25 billion samples at €0.12 per million
samples: €3.000</li> <li>2 billion samples at €0.11 per million samples: €220</li> </ul> <p><strong>Total:</strong> €6.650 per month with volume discounts instead of €7.800 without volume discounts.</p> <p>Upgrade your monitoring experience with Cockpit and enjoy smarter pricing that scales with your needs!</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#introducing-volumes-discounts-for-your-custom-metrics https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#introducing-volumes-discounts-for-your-custom-metrics observability <![CDATA[Cockpit | Transactional Email dashboard integration with Cockpit is
available]]> <p>Your monitoring experience just got even better with the addition of a Transactional Email dashboard in your Grafana.
Your email performance and reputation has never been easier or more intuitive!</p> <p>Gain insights into every aspect of your email delivery with a comprehensive array of metrics, including:</p> <ul> <li><strong>Current <a href="/transactional-email/concepts/#reputation-score">domain reputation</a>:</strong> Stay informed about your domain&#39;s reputation score.</li> <li><strong>Reputation score evolution:</strong> Track how your reputation score evolves, and make informed decisions to maintain or enhance it.
Read <a href="/transactional-email/reference-content/understanding-tem-reputation-score/">our dedicated documentation</a> to find out how.</li> <li><strong>Total emails sent:</strong> Keep track of the volume of emails your system is processing.</li> <li><strong>Total email drops:</strong> Identify delivery issues and take proactive measures to address them.</li> <li><strong>General activity overview:</strong> Get a bird&#39;s-eye view of your email activity trends over time.</li> <li><strong>Error activity:</strong> Pinpoint any errors or issues in your email delivery pipeline to solve them
swiftly.</li> <li><strong>Top recipient destinations:</strong> Understand who your emails reach the most.</li> <li><strong>Top bounce and fail recipients:</strong> Identify problematic recipients and take action to rectify issues.</li> </ul> <p>With these metrics at your disposal, optimizing your email delivery and ensuring a seamless user experience has never been simpler.
</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#transactional-email-dashboard-integration-with-cockpit-is-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#transactional-email-dashboard-integration-with-cockpit-is-available observability <![CDATA[Cockpit | Traces are now available!]]> <p>Traces are now available with Cockpit.</p> <ul> <li>Traces come with a retention period of 7 days in the free <a href="https://console.scaleway.com/cockpit/plans">pricing plan</a>.</li> <li>Traces are billed 0.35€ per GB ingested, per month.</li> </ul> <p>Get detailed records of your requests&#39;
behavior with traces, and enhance your monitoring capabilities!</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#traces-are-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#traces-are-now-available observability <![CDATA[Load-balancers | Public IPv6 addresses are now available for Load Balancer]]> <p>You can now add a public IPv6 address to both new and existing Load Balancers.
This feature is available for all regions, and IPv6 addresses can be attached in addition to existing IPv4 addresses.
Find out more in our <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/load-balancer/how-to/create-manage-flex-ips/">documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#public-ipv6-addresses-are-now-available-for-load-balancer https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#public-ipv6-addresses-are-now-available-for-load-balancer network <![CDATA[Webhosting | Web Hosting now available across all three regions!]]> <p>Our Web Hosting services are now accessible in all three regions: Paris, Amsterdam, and Warsaw.
</p> <p>Whether you are seeking reliability, speed, or localized service, you can now order your hosting from the location that best suits your needs.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#web-hosting-now-available-across-all-three-regions https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#web-hosting-now-available-across-all-three-regions managed-services <![CDATA[Webhosting | Web Hosting migration from Online.net to Scaleway Elements now available!]]> <p>A significant upgrade for all our clients is available now: the ability to seamlessly migrate your Web Hosting from Online to Scaleway
Elements.
Say hello to enhanced performance and features.</p> <p><strong>How it works:</strong></p> <p>The migration process starts off from your <a href="https://console.online.net/en/login">Online Console</a>.
Simply initiate the process when prompted, and you will be guided through each step.
If you have not already created a Scaleway account, you will receive an invitation to create one in the near future.</p> <p>Our migration tool handles everything for you, ensuring a smooth transition.
Plus, if you also have domains hosted with Scaleway/Online, the process becomes even more streamlined.
All you need to do is select your new hosting offer, and let the tool take care of the rest.</p> <p>Upgrade to Scaleway Elements today and experience the next level of Web Hosting!</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#web-hosting-migration-from-onlinenet-to-scaleway-elements-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#web-hosting-migration-from-onlinenet-to-scaleway-elements-now-available managed-services <![CDATA[Postgresql-and-mysql | Block Low Latency General Availability]]> <p>Block Storage Low Latency is available now in 5,000 IOPS or 15,000 IOPS flavors for the
most intensive workloads.
Available on DB-PLAY2, DB-PRO2, and DB-POP2 Instances in the PAR1, PAR2, AMS1, AMS3, and WAW3 Availability Zones.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#block-low-latency-general-availability https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#block-low-latency-general-availability databases <![CDATA[Postgresql-and-mysql | Complete DB-PRO2 offer available]]> <p>The latest generation of cost-optimized node types are now available: PRO2-XS, PRO2-S, PRO2-M, and PRO2-L.
These nodes boast cutting-edge hardware, offering superior performance compared to the DEV/GP node types.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#complete-db-pro2-offer-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#complete-db-pro2-offer-available databases <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Routed IPs are now default on Kubernetes clusters]]> <p><a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/news/routed-ips-are-coming-to-all-scaleway-products/">Routed IPs</a> are becoming default for all Scaleway resources.
Effective now, new Kubernetes clusters will provision nodes with public IP addresses directly routed to the Instance, without having to go through a NAT gateway to convert from public IP to a private IP.</p> <p>If you want to know more about the changes behind this evolution, have a look at our <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/blog/ip-mobility-removing-nat/">blog post</a>.</p> <p><a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/news/routed-ips-are-coming-to-all-scaleway-products/">Find out more about the manual and automatic migrations plans</a>.</p>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#routed-ips-are-now-default-on-kubernetes-clusters https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#routed-ips-are-now-default-on-kubernetes-clusters containers <![CDATA[Transactional-email | Transactional Email metrics are available in Cockpit!]]> <p>We are excited to announce that you can now view your Transactional Email metrics in a managed dashboard in Grafana!
Available through the Cockpit product, you can effortlessly monitor your email performance and the reputation of your domains.</p> <p>Access your dashboard via your domain&#39;s <strong>Metrics</strong> tab in the <a href="https://console.scaleway.com/transactional-email/domains/">Scaleway console.</a></p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#transactional-email-metrics-are-available-in-cockpit https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#transactional-email-metrics-are-available-in-cockpit managed-services <![CDATA[Transactional-email | Transactional Email DMARC policy]]> <p>After SPF,
DKIM, and MX, we are introducing the DMARC policy to provide further protection for your domains against identity theft and phishing attacks.
<a href="/transactional-email/how-to/authenticate-domain/#how-to-configure-dmarc-records">Read our dedicated documentation</a> to understand DMARC, and learn how to configure it.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#transactional-email-dmarc-policy https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#transactional-email-dmarc-policy managed-services <![CDATA[Transactional-email | XML attachments are now available!]]> <p>You can now send XML attachments in your transactional emails.
The following MIME types are supported:</p> <ul> <li><code>application/xml</code></li> <li><code>text/xml</code></li> </ul> <p>Find out about the full range of available attachment types in our <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/transactional-email/reference-content/tem-capabilities-and-limits/">documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#xml-attachments-are-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#xml-attachments-are-now-available managed-services <![CDATA[Gpu-instances | New GPU L40S Instances]]> <p>We are excited to introduce our latest L40S
GPU instances, delivering end-to-end acceleration for the next generation of AI-enabled applications.
Available in the Paris (PAR2) Region.</p> <p>Key features include:</p> <ul> <li>Nvidia L40S 48 GB (Ada Lovelace architecture)</li> <li>4th generation Tensor cores</li> <li>4th generation RT cores (graphics capability)</li> <li>Transformer Engine</li> <li>Available in multi-GPU 1, 2, 4 or 8 GPUs</li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-gpu-l40s-instances https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-gpu-l40s-instances compute <![CDATA[Nats | Resource quotas have been increased]]> <p>Scaleway Messaging and Queuing is evolving.
We have increased the limitations on our resource quotas, meaning that each Scaleway Project can now scale up to:</p> <ul> <li>50 SQS queues</li> <li>50 SNS topics</li> <li>50 NATS streams</li> </ul> <p>This update empowers you to handle larger workloads and enables more robust communication.</p> <p>For detailed information on the limitations and capabilities of our Messaging and Queuing protocols, please refer to the <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/organizations-and-projects/additional-content/organization-quotas/">documentation</a>.</p>

</p> <p>The Mac mini M1 (M1-M) range will stay on macOS 13 by default for now.
If you want to enjoy a smooth macOS 14 migration you can opt-in to our latest macOS configuration improvements by reinstalling your server through the console, then performing a manual upgrade using <code>softwareupdate</code>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#macos14-sonoma-is-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#macos14-sonoma-is-now-available bare-metal <![CDATA[Dedibox | Ubuntu 24.04 LTS (Noble Numbat) now available on Dedibox servers!]]> <p>You can now install the newly launched Ubuntu 24.04 LTS on your Dedibox server 🚀</p>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#ubuntu-2404-lts-noble-numbat-now-available-on-dedibox-servers https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#ubuntu-2404-lts-noble-numbat-now-available-on-dedibox-servers bare-metal <![CDATA[Elastic-metal | Ubuntu 24.04 LTS (Noble Numbat) now available on Elastic Metal servers!]]> <p>The latest Ubuntu 24.04 LTS is now available upon installation of your Elastic Metal server ✨</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#ubuntu-2404-lts-noble-numbat-now-available-on-elastic-metal-servers
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#ubuntu-2404-lts-noble-numbat-now-available-on-elastic-metal-servers bare-metal <![CDATA[Object-storage | Versioning activation at bucket creation]]> <p>It is now possible to activate bucket versioning for better resiliency directly when creating a bucket from the <a href="https://console.scaleway.com/">Scaleway console</a>.
As a reminder, versioning can be activated after creation or suspended at any time from the bucket settings in the console or via the CLI.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#versioning-activation-at-bucket-creation https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#versioning-activation-at-bucket-creation storage <![CDATA[Console | Console Search]]> <p>We are happy to announce that you can now find resources thanks to the new search in the console.
Find them by their name or ID.
More product integrations will come.
Refer to our <a href="/account/how-to/use-the-search-bar/">dedicated documentation</a> to see which products are available now.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#console-search https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#console-search console <![CDATA[Queues | Undelivered message queue feature is available]]> <p>When creating a new queue with Scaleway Queues, you can now configure a dead-letter queue to receive its on-hold or undelivered messages.
Read more in the <a href="/queues/concepts/#dead-letter-queue">documentation</a></p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#undelivered-message-queue-feature-is-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#undelivered-message-queue-feature-is-available serverless <![CDATA[Vpc | Network ACL is now available in Public Beta]]> <p>Network ACL allows you to define fine-grained access control policies for your network resources.
With Network ACL, you can better manage and restrict access between Private Networks.
Available for API and DevTools for now.
<a href="/vpc/reference-content/understanding-nacls/">Learn more about Network ACL.</a></p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#network-acl-is-now-available-in-public-beta https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#network-acl-is-now-available-in-public-beta network <![CDATA[Apple-silicon | Introduction of a new monthly configuration offering discounted prices]]> <p>We have introduced a monthly commitment plan for Apple silicon.
This is a new billing plan, offering better rates than pay-as-you-go pricing.</p> <p>The initial commitment is for one month from the server order.
At the end of your initial commitment period, the commitment is automatically renewed every month on the anniversary date unless you choose to delete the machine, in which case the deletion will take effect on the anniversary date of the commitment.</p> <p>Monthly pricing:</p> <ul> <li>M1-M: €75/month </li> <li>M2-M: €115/month</li> <li>M2-L: €159/month</li> <li>M4-S: €149/month</li> </ul> <p>Read <a href="/apple-silicon/how-to/manage-commitment-plan/">our documentation</a> on how to manage commitment plans.</p>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#introduction-of-a-new-monthly-configuration-offering-discounted-prices https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#introduction-of-a-new-monthly-configuration-offering-discounted-prices bare-metal <![CDATA[Public-gateways | New Public Gateway API v2]]> <p>We have released a new <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/developers/api/public-gateways/">Public Gateways API v2</a>, which comes with a new security feature for SSH bastion: <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/public-gateways/concepts/#allowed-ips">Allowed IPs</a>.
This version also removes some old DHCP features that are now managed by the IPAM and the built-in DHCP of the Private Networks.</p> <p>The API v1 is now deprecated and will be definitively removed by October 1, 2025.
Public Gateways users are encouraged to migrate to the API v2.
<a href="/public-gateways/reference-content/understanding-v2/">Learn more in our detailed documentation.</a></p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-public-gateway-api-v2 https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-public-gateway-api-v2 network <![CDATA[Generative-apis | Gemma 3 and Mistral Small 3.1 are now available]]> <p><a href="/generative-apis/reference-content/supported-models/#multimodal-models-chat-and-vision">Gemma 3 27B</a> and <a href="/generative-apis/reference-content/supported-models/#multimodal-models-chat-and-vision">Mistral Small 3.1 24B 2503</a> are now
available in <a href="/generative-apis/reference-content/model-lifecycle/">Preview</a> in Generative APIs.</p> <p>Both models are multimodal and support text generation and image analysis use cases.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#gemma-3-and-mistral-small-31-are-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#gemma-3-and-mistral-small-31-are-now-available ai-data <![CDATA[Transactional-email | Support for SVG attachments]]> <p>Transactional Email now supports <code>.svg</code> files as email attachments.
You can use them like any other supported file type.</p> <p>Check the full list of supported file extensions in the <a href="/transactional-email/reference-content/tem-capabilities-and-limits/#types-of-attachments-available">supported file types documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#support-for-svg-attachments https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#support-for-svg-attachments managed-services <![CDATA[Generative-apis | Custom models deployment available in beta]]> <p><a
href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/managed-inference/reference-content/supported-models/#custom-models">Custom models</a> can now be deployed on Managed Inference.<br><a href="https://console.scaleway.com/inference/models/import">Try it now</a> by providing a Hugging Face URL from a <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/managed-inference/reference-content/supported-models/#custom-models">compatible model</a>.
</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#custom-models-deployment-available-in-beta https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#custom-models-deployment-available-in-beta ai-data <![CDATA[Secret-manager | New behavior for secret deletion in Secret Manager]]> <p>When deleted, secrets and versions are now kept as &quot;scheduled for deletion&quot; for 7 days.
Secret versions scheduled for deletion are free.
They cannot be accessed during this period and can be restored for a cost of 1 cent per version.</p> <p>This feature protects infrastructures and applications from the impacts of accidental deletion of secrets or malicious actions.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-behavior-for-secret-deletion-in-secret-manager https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-behavior-for-secret-deletion-in-secret-manager security-identity <![CDATA[Product-catalog | The Product Catalog API is now available]]> <p>You can now have programmatic access to detailed product information including
specifications and pricing of the complete Scaleway product catalog.</p> <p>For more details, check out our <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/developers/api/product-catalog/public-catalog-api/">API documentation for Product Catalog</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#the-product-catalog-api-is-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#the-product-catalog-api-is-now-available billing <![CDATA[Gpu-instances | New GPU H100 SXM Instances available in PAR-2]]> <p>We are excited to introduce our latest H100-SXM GPU Instances, delivering exceptional performance,
scalability, and security for every workload.
H100 uses breakthrough innovations based on the NVIDIA Hopper architecture to deliver industry-leading conversational AI, speeding up large language models (LLMs).
Available in the Paris (PAR2) region.</p> <p>Key features include:</p> <ul> <li>Nvidia H100 SXM 80GB GB (Hopper architecture)</li> <li>4th generation Tensor cores</li> <li>4th generation NVlink, which offers 900 GB/s of GPU-to-GPU interconnect</li> <li>Transformer Engine</li> <li>Available immediately in 8 GPUs (2 and 4 GPUs per VM coming soon)</li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-gpu-h100-sxm-instances-available-in-par-2 https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-gpu-h100-sxm-instances-available-in-par-2 compute <![CDATA[Interlink | Scaleway InterLink is now
available in Public Beta!]]> <p>Scaleway InterLink is a new network product that offers a direct, private, fast, and secure connection between your infrastructure (on-premise, private cloud, or another public cloud) and your Scaleway VPC.
This connection bypasses the public internet, providing a reliable and low-latency connectivity solution.
<a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/interlink/">Read the documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#scaleway-interlink-is-now-available-in-public-beta https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#scaleway-interlink-is-now-available-in-public-beta network <![CDATA[Sql-databases | The SQL request size limit increased to 1,024 KB for Serverless SQL Databases]]> <p>The SQL request size limit for Serverless SQL Databases increased from 512 KB to 1,024 KB.
Note that responses to SQL queries are not limited in size and can exceed 1,024 KB.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#the-sql-request-size-limit-increased-to-1024-kb-for-serverless-sql-databases https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#the-sql-request-size-limit-increased-to-1024-kb-for-serverless-sql-databases serverless <![CDATA[Instances | Red Hat Enterprise Linux v9 & v10 (BYOS)]]> <p>We are pleased to announce that <strong>Red Hat Enterprise Linux (RHEL)</strong> versions 9 and 10 are now available as OS images for Scaleway Instances!</p> <p><strong>Bring Your Own
Subscription (BYOS)</strong></p> <p>RHEL on Scaleway operates on a Bring Your Own Subscription model.
You can <a href="/instances/reference-content/use-red-hat-license/">deploy a RHEL Instance directly from the Scaleway console</a> and activate it using your existing Red Hat subscription to receive updates, security patches, and support.</p> <p><strong>Prerequisites</strong></p> <ul> <li>An active Red Hat subscription eligible for Red Hat Cloud Access.</li> <li>A Red Hat Customer Portal account.</li> <li>Standard Scaleway account permissions (Owner or IAM permissions).</li> </ul> <p><strong>Important Notes</strong></p> <ul> <li><strong>Licensing:</strong> Instances are deployed unentitled.
You must register and attach your subscription to access Red Hat repositories.</li> <li><strong>Support:</strong> Technical support is provided directly through your Red Hat subscription, not via Scaleway Support.</li> <li><strong>Licenses:</strong> RHEL licenses must be ordered directly from Red Hat via the Customer Portal.</li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#red-hat-enterprise-linux-v9-and-v10-byos https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#red-hat-enterprise-linux-v9-and-v10-byos compute <![CDATA[Site-to-site-vpn | Site-to-Site VPN is now in General Availabilty]]>
<p>Scaleway Site-to-Site VPN is now in General Availability in every Region, via the Scaleway console, API, CLI, SDKs and Terraform.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#site-to-site-vpn-is-now-in-general-availabilty https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#site-to-site-vpn-is-now-in-general-availabilty network <![CDATA[Vpc-peering | VPC Peering is now in Public Beta]]> <p>Scaleway VPC Peering is now available in Public Beta in every Region, and can be accessed via the API, CLI, SDKs and Terraform.
Support for the Console is currently under development and will be available soon.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#vpc-peering-is-now-in-public-beta https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#vpc-peering-is-now-in-public-beta network <![CDATA[Audit-trail | Preconfigured alerts now available in Audit Trail]]> <p>You can now enable preconfigured alerts with Audit Trail events to get notified of abnormal behavior.
We added a first set of alert rules and will add more in the future.
Read <a href="/audit-trail/how-to/activate-adt-preconfigured-alerts/">our documentation</a> to find out how to manage your alerts.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#preconfigured-alerts-now-available-in-audit-trail https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#preconfigured-alerts-now-available-in-audit-trail monitoring <![CDATA[Audit-trail | Public Gateways events available in Audit Trail]]> <p>Public Gateways activity can now be tracked in Audit Trail.
Refer to <a href="/audit-trail/reference-content/adt-supported-endpoints/#public-gateways">our documentation</a> to see the list of available events.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#public-gateways-events-available-in-audit-trail https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#public-gateways-events-available-in-audit-trail monitoring <![CDATA[Containers | Serverless Containers VPC routing available]]> <p><strong>VPC Routing</strong> is now fully functional for Serverless Containers.
While attaching to a Private Network was previously possible, traffic can now flow seamlessly between your containers and other private resources within your VPC.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-containers-vpc-routing-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-containers-vpc-routing-available serverless <![CDATA[Generative-apis | More granular billing]]> <p>Generative APIs billing is performed in <strong>slices of 1,000 tokens</strong> (instead of the previous slices of 1,000,000 tokens) starting <strong>April 20th</strong>.
Prices <strong>do not</strong> change with this update.
After this change, for a similar token usage, any bill will remain <strong>the same or may be slightly lower</strong>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#more-granular-billing https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#more-granular-billing ai-data <![CDATA[Containers | Serverless Containers API v1 available]]> <p>The Scaleway Serverless Containers API is now generally available as <code>v1</code> (previously <code>v1beta1</code>, which is still available).
This stable release includes significant improvements and lays the foundation for upcoming advanced features.</p> <p>Console, CLI, Terraform, and other tools maintained by Scaleway will be gradually updated.</p> <p>For details, see <a href="/serverless-containers/reference-content/v1-migration-guide/">the dedicated v1 documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-containers-api-v1-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-containers-api-v1-available serverless <![CDATA[Elastic-metal | New Beryllium offers]]> <p>New Epyc 4005 servers are
now available in the Beryllium range with the specifications listed below.</p> <table> <thead> <tr> <th>Offer</th> <th>CPU</th> <th>RAM</th> <th>Storage</th> <th>Bandwidth</th> <th>Price</th> </tr> </thead> <tbody><tr> <td>EM-B130E-NVMe</td> <td>AMD EPYC 4245P (6C / 12T - 3.9 GHz)</td> <td>32 to 64 GB RAM</td> <td>2 x 1.02 TB NVMe</td> <td>3 to 10 Gbps bandwidth</td> <td><a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/pricing/elastic-metal/">See pricing page</a></td> </tr> <tr> <td>EM-B230E-NVMe</td> <td>AMD EPYC 4345P (8C / 16T - 3.8 GHz)</td> <td>64 to 128 GB RAM</td> <td>2 x 1.02 TB NVMe</td> <td>3 to

href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/pricing/elastic-metal/">See pricing page</a></td> </tr> </tbody></table> <p>The servers are available in the following regions: PAR-1, PAR-2, and AMS-1.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-beryllium-offers https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-beryllium-offers bare-metal <![CDATA[Generative-apis | Responses API is now generally available]]> <p>The <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/developers/api/generative-apis/responses">Responses API</a> is now generally available.
The Responses API is recommended for use only with the <code>gpt-oss-120b</code> model.
For more information, see the <a href="/managed-inference/reference-content/openai-compatibility/">Chat Completions and Responses API comparison</a>.
</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#responses-api-is-now-generally-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#responses-api-is-now-generally-available ai-data <![CDATA[Environmental-footprint | Discover the environmental impact of your Managed Database]]> <p>Our Environmental Footprint Calculator now supports Managed Databases (PostgreSQL &amp; MySQL, Redis, and MongoDB) Designed to go beyond simple accounting, it provides real usage-based impact metrics, covering both CO₂ emissions and water consumption.
This multi-criteria approach helps you understand, compare, and reduce the footprint of your cloud infrastructure.
Learn more in our document about <a href="/environmental-footprint/additional-content/managed-databases-env-footprint/">Managed Database footprint calculation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#discover-the-environmental-impact-of-your-managed-database https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#discover-the-environmental-impact-of-your-managed-database billing <![CDATA[Generative-apis | Managed Inference becomes Generative APIs - Dedicated Deployment]]> <p>Starting <strong>1 May 2026</strong>, Managed Inference becomes <strong>Generative APIs - Dedicated Deployment</strong>.
All APIs, existing resources, pricing, and SLAs <strong>remain unchanged</strong>.
All Managed Inference related content (such as documentation or Cockpit dashboards) will gradually be renamed <strong>Generative APIs - Dedicated</strong>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#managed-inference-becomes-generative-apis-dedicated-deployment https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#managed-inference-becomes-generative-apis-dedicated-deployment ai-data <![CDATA[Postgresql-and-mysql | Managed Database now support PostgreSQL 15]]> <p>This PostgreSQL release brings many improvements.
For more information, refer to the <a href="https://www.postgresql.org/about/news/postgresql-153-148-1311-1215-and-1120-released-2637/">PostgreSQL release notes</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#managed-database-now-support-postgresql-15 https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#managed-database-now-support-postgresql-15 databases <![CDATA[Postgresql-and-mysql | New Cost-optimized and Production-optimized offers]]> <p>Our Managed Databases offer is complemented with some new Cost-Optimized nodes and the new Production-Optimized range.</p>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-cost-optimized-and-production-optimized-offers https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-cost-optimized-and-production-optimized-offers databases <![CDATA[Redistm | Managed database for Redis now supports the default username]]> <p>You can now use the <code>default</code> username on your managed database for Redis.
This enables some software integrations where specific usernames are not supported.
Anonymous connection remains unavailable.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#managed-database-for-redis-now-supports-the-default-username https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#managed-database-for-redis-now-supports-the-default-username databases <![CDATA[Cockpit | Cockpit feature request is completed]]> <p>We are happy to announce that we closed one of the <a href="https://feature-request.scaleway.com/posts/38/log-centralization-and-management">most wanted features</a> at Scaleway (open since Jan.
21).
Almost every Scaleway products are available in Cockpit for metrics and/or logs.
New features and capabilities will be added to the product.
Refer to our <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/cockpit/reference-content/cockpit-product-integration">product documentation</a> to see what is already available or planned.</p> <p>For all future feature requirements, we encourage you to open a specific feature request of what you need to integrate into Cockpit.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cockpit-feature-request-is-completed https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cockpit-feature-request-is-completed observability <![CDATA[Instances | New Availability Zone WAW-3]]> <p>We are excited to introduce our latest
Availability Zone (AZ), WAW-3, located in Warsaw, Poland.</p> <p>WAW-3 is now fully operational, providing enhanced accessibility and proximity to support our customers in the Central European region.
This comes as the latest development in our commitment to offer an ever-resilient and high-performing cloud infrastructure.
Deploy your Instances in WAW-3 now to experience unmatched performance, reliability, and data sovereignty.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-availability-zone-waw-3 https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-availability-zone-waw-3 compute <![CDATA[Topics-and-events | SQS and SNS namespace creation]]> <p>To pave the way for the new Messaging and Queuing API, it is no longer possible to create more than one SQS/SNS namespace per Project.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#sqs-and-sns-namespace-creation
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#sqs-and-sns-namespace-creation serverless <![CDATA[Queues | SQS and SNS namespace creation]]> <p>To pave the way for the new Messaging and Queuing API, it is no longer possible to create more than one SQS/SNS namespace per Project.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#sqs-and-sns-namespace-creation https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#sqs-and-sns-namespace-creation serverless <![CDATA[Postgresql-and-mysql | Read Replica promotion is now available!]]> <p>You can now promote a Read Replica node to a standalone Database Instance.
This is useful for database failure recovery or sharding.</p> <p>For more information, refer to the <a href="/managed-databases-for-postgresql-and-mysql/how-to/manage-read-replicas/#how-to-promote-a-read-replica">How to manage Read Replica</a> documentation page.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#read-replica-promotion-is-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#read-replica-promotion-is-now-available databases <![CDATA[Postgresql-and-mysql | Encryption-at-rest available in Public Beta]]> <p>Encryption-at-rest can now be activated on new PostgreSQL &amp; MySQL
instances.
Read <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/managed-databases-for-postgresql-and-mysql/concepts/#encryption-at-rest">this documentation</a> for more information.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#encryption-at-rest-available-in-public-beta https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#encryption-at-rest-available-in-public-beta databases <![CDATA[Public-gateways | New PGW-L offer]]> <p>We have just released the <strong>Public Gateway L</strong>, a new offer with up to 3 Gbps of bandwidth for your most network-intensive workloads.
Egress is included and it is available in all regions.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-pgw-l-offer https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-pgw-l-offer network <![CDATA[Containers | Serverless Containers more CPU and RAM]]> <p>Serverless Containers now support higher CPU and RAM limits, allowing for more demanding workloads.
This update provides more power and flexibility to meet your application&#39;s needs.</p> <p>In addition, we have introduced a balanced resource allocation system, establishing a relationship between RAM and CPU to help you select optimal resource combinations.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-containers-more-cpu-and-ram https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-containers-more-cpu-and-ram serverless <![CDATA[Containers | Deprecation of the serverless_containers_requests_total metric]]> <p>Following the introduction of the
<code>serverless_containers_requests_per_second</code> metric, we are deprecating the <code>serverless_containers_requests_total</code> metric for use in custom dashboards and alerts.
We recommend that you update your custom dashboards and alerts to use the new <code>serverless_containers_requests_per_second</code> metric instead.
The removal will be effective on September 19, 2024.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deprecation-of-the-serverlesscontainersrequeststotal-metric https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deprecation-of-the-serverlesscontainersrequeststotal-metric serverless <![CDATA[Functions | Deprecation of the serverless_functions_requests_total metric]]> <p>Following the introduction of the <code>serverless_functions_requests_per_second</code> metric, we are deprecating the <code>serverless_functions_requests_total</code> metric for use in custom dashboards and alerts.
We recommend that you update your custom dashboards and alerts to use the new <code>serverless_functions_requests_per_second</code> metric instead.
The removal will be effective on September 19, 2024.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deprecation-of-the-serverlessfunctionsrequeststotal-metric https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deprecation-of-the-serverlessfunctionsrequeststotal-metric serverless <![CDATA[Jobs | Metrics available on Serverless Jobs]]> <p>Metrics are now available for advanced monitoring of Serverless Jobs performances.

https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#quota-consumption-is-now-displayed-at-organization-level https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#quota-consumption-is-now-displayed-at-organization-level console <![CDATA[Generative-apis | Chat and Embedding APIs now return usage stats]]> <p>Managed Inference endpoints now return usage stats (number of tokens in prompt, completion and totals) both for streaming and non-streaming responses.</p> <p>For streaming responses, the usage field is incremented in each chunk, and completed in the very last chunk of the response.</p> <p>More details and

possibilities and higher performance for your deployments.

https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#runc-bumped-to-1112-for-cve-2024-21626 containers <![CDATA[Containers | Deprecation of "/logs" endpoint]]> <p>Due to infrequent usage and changes affecting the storage of user logs, we are announcing the deprecation of the <code>/logs</code> endpoint (or <code>ListLogs</code> for our SDK users).
However, users can still access their container logs conveniently via their <a href="/cockpit/quickstart/">Cockpit interface</a>.
A dedicated dashboard called &quot;Serverless Containers Logs&quot; is accessible there.
For detailed instructions, refer to our <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/developers/api/serverless-containers/#logs">documentation</a>.
For users who are already using Cockpit for log access (logs have been directed to Cockpit for the past 6 months), there will be no disruption.
Note, the <code>/logs</code> endpoint will be fully deprecated in one month, effective March 12, 2024.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deprecation-of-logs-endpoint https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deprecation-of-logs-endpoint serverless <![CDATA[Functions | Deprecation of "/logs" endpoint]]> <p>Due to infrequent usage and changes affecting the storage of user logs, we are announcing the deprecation of the <code>/logs</code> endpoint (or <code>ListLogs</code> for our SDK users).

Note, the <code>/logs</code> endpoint will be fully deprecated in one month, effective March 12, 2024.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deprecation-of-logs-endpoint https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deprecation-of-logs-endpoint serverless <![CDATA[Webhosting | Dedicated IP for Web Hosting]]> <p>Web Hosting with dedicated IP is now available!

<a href="/webhosting/how-to/order-dedicated-ip/">Enable your dedicated IP</a> during your hosting order or later via the console, and we will provide you with a dedicated IP linked directly to your hosting plan.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#dedicated-ip-for-web-hosting https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#dedicated-ip-for-web-hosting managed-services <![CDATA[Webhosting | Email forwarding enabled (Scaleway Elements Web Hosting)]]> <p>Email Forwarding is now available to all our Web Hosting (Scaleway Elements) customers.
This feature allows you to seamlessly forward incoming emails from your professional Web Hosting email address to your personal inbox.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#email-forwarding-enabled-scaleway-elements-web-hosting https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#email-forwarding-enabled-scaleway-elements-web-hosting managed-services <![CDATA[Kubernetes | DNS fix to solve occasional resolution failures]]> <p>The DNS resolution issue has been resolved.
To implement the fix:</p> <ul> <li>Replace or reboot your nodes.</li> <li>Use a fully qualified domain name (FQDN) for resolving addresses within the Private Network.
For example, if you have a service named <code>foo</code> in the Private Network <code>mypn</code>, use <code>foo.mypn</code>.</li> </ul> <Message type="important"> - Avoid using existing [top-level domains (TLDs)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Top-level_domain) as Private Network names to prevent potential issues.
For instance, naming your PN `fr` may cause problems resolving external addresses like `google.fr`.
- Be aware that `prod` and `dev` are [valid TLDs](https://data.iana.org/TLD/tlds-alpha-by-domain.txt).
</Message> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#dns-fix-to-solve-occasional-resolution-failures https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#dns-fix-to-solve-occasional-resolution-failures containers <![CDATA[Object-storage | Object Storage introduces bucket use case to customize your experience]]> <p>Upon creation of a bucket on the Scaleway console, you can fill in its use case.

</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#messaging-and-queuing-is-now-available-in-the-ams-region https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#messaging-and-queuing-is-now-available-in-the-ams-region serverless <![CDATA[Topics-and-events | Messaging and Queuing is now available in the AMS region!]]> <p>You can now manage your Messaging and Queuing resources, including SNS, in the AMS region.
For more information, refer to our <a href="/topics-and-events/">documentation</a>.
</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#messaging-and-queuing-is-now-available-in-the-ams-region https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#messaging-and-queuing-is-now-available-in-the-ams-region serverless <![CDATA[Topics-and-events | SNS Subscriptions creation available in the console]]> <p>You can now manage your SNS subscriptions <a href="https://console.scaleway.com/messaging/protocols/">directly from the Scaleway console</a>.
You can subscribe to HTTP/S endpoints and Serverless Compute resources (Functions &amp; Containers).
You can refer to our documentation to find out <a href="/topics-and-events/how-to/create-manage-subscriptions/">how to create and manage SNS subscriptions</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#sns-subscriptions-creation-available-in-the-console https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#sns-subscriptions-creation-available-in-the-console serverless <![CDATA[Secret-manager | A new version of the Secret Manager API is available]]> <p>Scaleway&#39;s Secret Manager has a new API version available: <code>v1beta1</code>!
This new version comes with major improvements:</p> <ul> <li>Improved resilience and scalability</li> <li>No more folder endpoints, but still the possibility to use paths to organize secrets</li> <li>Changing the path of a secret has become simpler</li> </ul> <p>The Scaleway console now uses this version for the Secret Manager product pages.
For more information, you can refer to the <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/developers/api/secret-manager">API documentation</a>.</p> <p>Note that the <code>v1alpha1</code> API is still supported but it will eventually be deprecated.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#a-new-version-of-the-secret-manager-api-is-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#a-new-version-of-the-secret-manager-api-is-available security-identity <![CDATA[Billing | New billing space]]> <p><strong>New Billing Space available!</strong></p> <p>Our brand-new Billing Space is now live and ready
for you to explore.
Accessible right from the console&#39;s right-hand menu, this space is designed to streamline your billing experience.
Here is what is in for you:</p> <ul> <li><strong>Overview Page</strong>: Your go-to destination for essential information on managing your usage and invoices.</li> <li><strong>Consumption Management</strong>: Track your budget alerts and soon, the Cost Manager tool for cost tracking.</li> <li><strong>Optimization Page</strong>: Access vouchers and upcoming cost optimization offers.</li> <li><strong>Billing and Payments Information</strong>: A dedicated section for all your billing and payment details.</li> </ul> <p>Explore these new features today and manage your billing more efficiently than
ever before!</p> <pre><code>&lt;Message type=&quot;tip&quot;&gt; Refer to the [Billing documentation](/billing/) for more information.
&lt;/Message&gt; </code></pre> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-billing-space https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-billing-space billing <![CDATA[Instances | OpenSSH server now preinstalled on Windows 2022 images]]> <p>The latest release of <strong>Windows Server 2022</strong> and <strong>Windows Server 2022 Core</strong> images now includes the <strong>OpenSSH Server</strong> and <strong>OpenSSH Client</strong> packages by default.
The <strong>OpenSSH Server</strong> is installed but <strong>disabled by default</strong> for security reasons.
To enable it at Instance creation, add the tag <code>with-ssh</code> when launching your Instance.
<a href="/instances/how-to/enable-openssh-windows/">Read the full documentation for more details</a> </p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#openssh-server-now-preinstalled-on-windows-2022-images https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#openssh-server-now-preinstalled-on-windows-2022-images compute <![CDATA[Key-manager | Key Manager is in Public Beta!]]> <p>Scaleway Key Manager is a service that allows you to create, manage, and use cryptographic keys in a centralized and secure service.</p> <p>Key Manager helps organizations achieve secure key management by handling low-level and
error-prone cryptographic details for you.</p> <p>Find out more about Key Manager in the <a href="/key-manager/">dedicated documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#key-manager-is-in-public-beta https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#key-manager-is-in-public-beta security-identity <![CDATA[Generative-apis | New models preview DeepSeek R1 and DeepSeek R1 Distilled Llama 70B]]> <p><a href="/generative-apis/reference-content/supported-models/#request-a-model">DeepSeek R1</a> and <a href="/generative-apis/reference-content/supported-models/#request-a-model">DeepSeek
R1 Distilled Llama 70B</a> are now available in <a href="/generative-apis/reference-content/model-lifecycle/">Preview</a> on Generative APIs.</p> <p>DeepSeek R1 is an open-weight reasoning model matching proprietary models performance.
Distilled version improves Llama model performance on reasoning tasks like mathematics or code.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-models-preview-deepseek-r1-and-deepseek-r1-distilled-llama-70b https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-models-preview-deepseek-r1-and-deepseek-r1-distilled-llama-70b ai-data <![CDATA[Generative-apis | New model preview DeepSeek R1 Distilled Llama 70B]]> <p><a href="/managed-inference/reference-content/model-catalog/#deepseek-r1-distill-llama-70b">DeepSeek R1 Distilled Llama 70B</a> is now available on Managed Inference.</p> <p>DeepSeek R1
Distilled Llama improves Llama model performance on reasoning use cases like mathematics or code.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-model-preview-deepseek-r1-distilled-llama-70b https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-model-preview-deepseek-r1-distilled-llama-70b ai-data <![CDATA[Transactional-email | New permission sets for email creation via SMTP or the Scaleway API]]> <p>With these new permission sets, any user or service can create transactional emails either through SMTP or the Scaleway Transactional Email API.</p> <ul>
<li><code>TransactionalEmailEmailSmtpCreate</code>: Grants the ability to create emails via SMTP.</li> <li><code>TransactionalEmailEmailApiCreate</code>: Grants the ability to create emails via the API.</li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-permission-sets-for-email-creation-via-smtp-or-the-scaleway-api https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-permission-sets-for-email-creation-via-smtp-or-the-scaleway-api managed-services <![CDATA[Containers | Container CRON trigger validation rules]]> <p>Issues with validation rules for CRON creation via the Scaleway <a

You will then be redirected automatically to the Llama 3.3 70B API afterwards.
Llama 3.1 8B is not affected by this change and remains supported.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deprecation-llama-31-70b https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deprecation-llama-31-70b ai-data <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Kubernetes data plane logs in Cockpit]]> <p><strong>Centralized monitoring is now available</strong>, allowing you to send Kubernetes container logs to Cockpit for streamlined monitoring.
Setup is easy with <strong>one-click deployment</strong> via Easy Deploy using Promtail.
This feature captures <strong>all container logs</strong>, including Pod stdout/stderr and systemd journal.
Additionally, you can control ingestion costs with <strong>customizable filtering options</strong>.</p> <p>Learn more in our dedicated documentation: <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/kubernetes/how-to/monitor-data-plane-with-cockpit/">Monitor Data Plane with Cockpit</a></p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#kubernetes-data-plane-logs-in-cockpit https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#kubernetes-data-plane-logs-in-cockpit containers <![CDATA[Containers | Improved pricing transparency]]> <p>To improve clarity about Serverless Containers costs, we have added pricing details
to the container overview page.</p> <p>Additionally, you now get a breakdown of pricing changes when modifying resources.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#improved-pricing-transparency https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#improved-pricing-transparency serverless <![CDATA[Containers | Serverless Containers custom health checks]]> <p>This feature allows you to define <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/serverless-containers/concepts/#health-check">custom health checks</a> for your Serverless Containers, enabling more precise monitoring and improved reliability.</p>

</p> <p>Your container can now scale up or down based on CPU or RAM usage to better adapt to your specific workloads.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-containers-new-autoscaling-rules https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-containers-new-autoscaling-rules serverless <![CDATA[Environmental-footprint | Environmental Footprint Calculator now available for Apple silicon servers!]]> <p>Scaleway’s Environmental Footprint Calculator now supports Apple silicon dedicated servers.

Monthly environmental reports, including CO₂ emissions and water usage, are also available.</p> <p>Start tracking your impact today!
Check out your environmental reports in the <a href="https://console.scaleway.com">Scaleway console</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#environmental-footprint-calculator-now-available-for-apple-silicon-servers https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#environmental-footprint-calculator-now-available-for-apple-silicon-servers environmental-footprint <![CDATA[Functions | Serverless Functions pagination fix]]> <p>Pagination for Projects with more than 10 Serverless Functions namespaces was dysfunctional on the Scaleway console.</p> <p>The issue has now been fixed.</p>

Read <a href="/audit-trail/reference-content/adt-supported-endpoints/#vpc">our documentation</a> to see the list of events available.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#vpc-events-available-in-audit-trail https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#vpc-events-available-in-audit-trail monitoring <![CDATA[Iam | SCIM user-provisioning in Early Access]]> <p>SCIM user-provisioning is now available for some Organizations.
Combined with SAML, SCIM allows external Identity Providers to manage user creation, updates and authentication.
<a href="/iam/how-to/manage-users-with-scim/">Read our documentation to learn more</a></p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#scim-user-provisioning-in-early-access https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#scim-user-provisioning-in-early-access security-identity <![CDATA[Postgresql-and-mysql | Get Database Instance metrics API endpoint deprecated]]> <p>The <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/developers/api/managed-databases-for-postgresql-and-mysql/#path-database-instances-deprecated-get-database-instance-metrics">Get Database Instance metrics endpoint</a> is now deprecated.
It will be removed on March 10th.
All Database Instance metrics are available <a href="/managed-databases-for-postgresql-and-mysql/how-to/monitor-databases-cockpit/">in Cockpit</a> and can be retrieved <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/cockpit/reference-content/cockpit-supported-endpoints/#mimir-query-endpoints">via the Cockpit API</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#get-database-instance-metrics-api-endpoint-deprecated https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#get-database-instance-metrics-api-endpoint-deprecated databases <![CDATA[Webhosting | Logcenter and Stats on Dedibox Webhosting]]> <p>As part
of ongoing infrastructure improvements, the Logcenter and Stats services for Dedibox Webhosting are being deprecated.</p> <p>The impacted endpoints are <code>logcenter.online.net</code> and <code>stats.online.net</code>.</p> <p>If you are currently using these services, we invite you to migrate your hosting to Scaleway to continue benefiting from logging and statistics features in our updated environment.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#logcenter-and-stats-on-dedibox-webhosting https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#logcenter-and-stats-on-dedibox-webhosting
domains-and-webhosting <![CDATA[Domains-and-dns | .it TLD now available]]> <p>Scaleway now supports <strong>.it</strong> domain name creation, renewal, and transfer.

Follow <a href="/managed-databases-for-redis/api-cli/upgrade-redis-version/">this procedure</a> to upgrade to the latest version (8.4.0).</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#redis-v7211-deprecated https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#redis-v7211-deprecated databases <![CDATA[Apple-silicon | Asahi Linux launch with Mac mini M2 Pro]]> <p>Native Linux support is now available on Apple Silicon via bare-metal Asahi Linux installations.
Initial hardware support is provided for Mac mini M2 Pro.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#asahi-linux-launch-with-mac-mini-m2-pro https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#asahi-linux-launch-with-mac-mini-m2-pro bare-metal <![CDATA[Jobs | Serverless Jobs default injected variables]]> <p>Serverless Jobs now offers a list of injected environment variables to help you build automation faster.
New variables are using <code>SCW_SLS</code> prefix.
<a href="/serverless-jobs/reference-content/injected-environment-variables/">Read more</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-jobs-default-injected-variables https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-jobs-default-injected-variables serverless <![CDATA[Jobs | Serverless Jobs - API version upgrade]]> <p>The Serverless Jobs API changed from <code>v1alpha1</code> to <code>v1alpha2</code>, and now offers new features:</p> <ul> <li>The <code>command</code> field has been upgraded to <code>startup_command</code> and <code>args</code> for improved flexibility and
more use cases</li> <li>The <code>reason</code> field was added to give more context about how a job is terminated</li> </ul> <p>For more details about the changes, <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/serverless-jobs/reference-content/v1alpha1-to-v1alpha2/">read our dedicated documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-jobs-api-version-upgrade https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-jobs-api-version-upgrade serverless <![CDATA[Containers | Serverless framework package upgrade v0.5.0]]> <p>Users of <a
href="https://github.com/scaleway/serverless-scaleway-functions">Scaleway Serverless Framework plugin</a> (<code>serverless-scaleway-functions</code> npm package) should upgrade to version <strong>0.5.0</strong> to ensure compatibility with this change.
The latest release includes updated behaviour to handle the deprecation gracefully.</p> <p>Please, update your dependency: </p> <pre><code class="language-bash">npm i serverless-scaleway-functions@~0.5.0 </code></pre> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-framework-package-upgrade-v050 https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-framework-package-upgrade-v050 serverless <![CDATA[Containers | Deprecation of the redeploy parameter in the UpdateContainer endpoint]]> <p><strong>Deprecation of the <code>redeploy: false</code> parameter in the <code>UpdateContainer</code>
endpoint</strong> </p> <p>The <code>redeploy: false</code> option in the <code>UpdateContainer</code> API (<code>PATCH /containers/v1beta1/regions/{region}/containers/{container_id}</code>) is now deprecated.
This parameter was previously used to prevent redeployment of containers when no configuration changes occurred.
Going forward, the API will systematically redeploy containers when necessary, and any use of <code>redeploy: false</code> will be ignored.</p> <p>We strongly advise against relying on this field in your automation or deployment workflows.
If you need to force a redeployment regardless of configuration changes, please use the dedicated <code>DeployContainer</code> method instead.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deprecation-of-the-redeploy-parameter-in-the-updatecontainer-endpoint https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deprecation-of-the-redeploy-parameter-in-the-updatecontainer-endpoint serverless <![CDATA[Gpu-instances | Next-generation GPU Instances are available]]> <p>Paired with one of the best processors &#8211; the AMD EPYC™ 7003 series, GPU-3070 is built on the world-class NVIDIA Universal GPU,
providing you with strong computing powers for your intensive workloads.
The GPU-3070 Instances are now available in our <code>PAR2</code> region.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#next-generation-gpu-instances-are-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#next-generation-gpu-instances-are-available compute <![CDATA[Iam | IAM solution is now in private beta]]> <p>Scaleway IAM (Identity and Access Management) is a free service providing you access control with flexibility across Scaleway cloud resources, securing your infrastructure in the easiest way.</p> <Message type="note"> Due to the high demand for IAM, the Private Beta phase is
currently only open to selected users identified by the Product team.
Fill out this form to request your access: https://scaleway.typeform.com/to/pZrr5Rei </Message> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#iam-solution-is-now-in-private-beta https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#iam-solution-is-now-in-private-beta iam <![CDATA[Iam | Project API is now public!]]> <p>Projects can be created, managed and deleted from the CLI, Terraform and other developer tools maintained by Scaleway.</p> <p>IAM must be used to generate an API key with the permissions to manage Projects.</p> <p>Find out more on the <a
href="/organizations-and-projects/api-cli/managing-projects/">Managing projects using Scaleway APIs</a> documentation page.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#project-api-is-now-public https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#project-api-is-now-public iam <![CDATA[Iam | Identity and Access Management (IAM) is now in General Availability!]]> <p>IAM allows you to share access to the management of your Scaleway resources, in a controlled and secure manner.
</p> <p>You can now create and manage policies to give fine-grained access rights to Scaleway users, applications and groups.
API keys can be attached to a user or an application and inherit their rights.</p> <p>IAM features are available though Console, CLI and Terraform.</p> <p>For Organizations created before December 5th, IAM can be activated by the Owner of the Organization on the console.
Accounts created since December 5th 2022 have IAM activated by default.</p> <p>Find out more in our dedicated <a href="/iam/reference-content/overview/">IAM documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#identity-and-access-management-iam-is-now-in-general-availability https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#identity-and-access-management-iam-is-now-in-general-availability iam <![CDATA[Instances | New images are available for Instances]]> <p>The following new images are available for your Instances:</p> <ul> <li>Ubuntu 22.04 Jammy Jellyfish</li> <li>GitLab
InstantApp</li> <li>Nextcloud InstantApp</li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-images-are-available-for-instances https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-images-are-available-for-instances compute <![CDATA[Instances | New OS images available]]> <p>We released <strong>Rocky Linux 9</strong> and <strong>AlmaLinux 9</strong> OS images.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-os-images-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-os-images-available compute <![CDATA[Instances | New OS images available]]> <p>We released a fix for VPC related
issues on the following operating systems:</p> <ul> <li>Centos Stream 9</li> <li>Fedora 36</li> <li>AlmaLinux 8</li> <li>Rocky Linux 8</li> <li>Debian Buster &amp; Debian Bullseye</li> </ul> <p>Users wanting to get the patch without having to launch new Instances can run the following commands:</p> <ul> <li>For Ubuntu and Debian: <code>apt-get install scaleway-ecosystem</code></li> <li>For CentOS, Fedora...: <code>dnf update scaleway-ecosystem</code></li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-os-images-available
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-os-images-available compute <![CDATA[Instances | All virtual Instance images have been updated]]> <p>All virtual Instance images have been updated with the latest version of the <code>scaleway-ecosystem</code> (0.0.5-3) which fixes various issues related to Private Networks such as:</p> <ul> <li>Absence of configuration of the Private Network interface</li> <li>Misconfiguration of the Private Network interface</li> <li>The Private Network does not come up when hot-plugged on Ubuntu Focal</li> </ul>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#all-virtual-instance-images-have-been-updated https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#all-virtual-instance-images-have-been-updated compute <![CDATA[Instances | Security Groups support rules configuration in a single API call]]> <p>It is now possible to set/get all the Security Group rules in a single API call instead of having to manage them individually.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#security-groups-support-rules-configuration-in-a-single-api-call
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#security-groups-support-rules-configuration-in-a-single-api-call compute <![CDATA[Instances | A new generation of production-grade Instances available]]> <p>Build your infrastructure on state-of-the-art AMD EPYC™ 7003 Series processors at an amazing price/performance ratio.
<a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/cost-optimized-instances/">Learn more about PRO2 Instances.</a></p> <p>PRO2 Instances are now available in:</p> <ul> <li><code>fr-par-1</code></li> <li><code>fr-par-2</code></li> <li><code>nl-ams-2</code></li> <li><code>pl-waw-1</code></li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#a-new-generation-of-production-grade-instances-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#a-new-generation-of-production-grade-instances-available compute <![CDATA[Instances | End of Life for some OS images and InstantApps]]> <p>The following Instances images
have reached their end of live and have been removed:</p> <ul> <li>Ubuntu 16.04 Xenial Xerus</li> <li>Centos 7.6</li> <li>CentOS 8</li> <li>Fedora 32</li> <li>OwnCloud Instant App</li> </ul> <Message type="important"> Scaleway does not guarantee maintenance or troubleshooting on those Instances images.
</Message> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#end-of-life-for-some-os-images-and-instantapps https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#end-of-life-for-some-os-images-and-instantapps compute <![CDATA[Instances | Boot on Block is now available for all Instances]]> <p>All Instance offers, including DEV, GP, RENDER, and Stardust can now boot on Block volumes.
This type of storage is safer and can be moved from one Instance to another.
Make sure you check our <a href="/instances/how-to/create-an-instance/">documentation</a> to create an Instance booting on a block volume or to <a href="/instances/how-to/use-boot-modes/#how-to-change-the-boot-volume">change the boot volume</a> of your Instance.</p> <p>For API users, note that the local volume <code>min_size</code> returned by a call to <code>/instance/v1/zones/{zone}/products/servers</code> is now <code>0</code>, whereas it was previously equal to the <code>max_size</code>.</p> <p>Be careful if you are using this value in your automation scripts, as trying to create an
Instance with a local volume of size <code>0</code> will fail.</p> <Message type="important"> This impacts our Terraform provider.
We have currently implemented a workaround, which will be deprecated when the next release of Terraform is available.
Make sure to keep your Terraform up to date.
</Message> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#boot-on-block-is-now-available-for-all-instances https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#boot-on-block-is-now-available-for-all-instances compute <![CDATA[Instances | New release of the Debian Bullseye OS image available!]]> <p>We released a fix for the Debian Bullseye OS image to avoid an issue when updating the operating system.
This fix is immediately available for DEV1/GP1/PLAY2/PRO2 Instances.
For ENT1 Instances, we are working with Debian to include the bug fixes.
Please watch the changelog for the update.
</p> <p>If you encounter an issue with the Debian Bullseye OS image after updating the operating-system (error: <code>filesystem is mounted as read-only</code>), follow our <a href="/instances/troubleshooting/reboot-from-faulty-kernel/">documentation</a> to boot your Instance in recovery mode and recover to a working kernel version.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-release-of-the-debian-bullseye-os-image-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-release-of-the-debian-bullseye-os-image-available compute <![CDATA[Instances | New generation of Development
Instances available - PLAY2]]> <p>A new generation of Development Instances is now available: PLAY2.
Scaleway’s PLAY2 Instances are designed for testing and development purposes.
PLAY2 Instances are the second generation of <a href="/instances/concepts/#development-instance">Development Instances</a> after DEV1.
They are small, agile and meet performance requirements for standard testing and development scenarios.
<a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/cost-optimized-instances/">Learn more about PLAY2 Instances</a> on their dedicated product page.
PLAY2 Instances are currently available in:</p> <ul> <li>fr-par-2</li> <li>nl-ams-2</li> <li>pl-waw-1 More AZs will follow in the coming weeks.</li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-generation-of-development-instances-available-play2 https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-generation-of-development-instances-available-play2 compute <![CDATA[Instances | PLAY2 Instances are available in par-1]]> <p>The new generation of PLAY2 development Instances is now also available in <code>par-1</code>.</p>
