will now silently drop messages coming from their own hub events.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#iot-hub-route-updated https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#iot-hub-route-updated managed-services <![CDATA[Instances | New ENT1-XXS and ENT1-XS offers available in the ENT1 range]]> <p>New offers are available for Scaleway <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/production-optimized-instances/">Enterprise Instances</a>.
</p> <p>Joining the ENT1 range, <strong>ENT1-XXS</strong> and <strong>ENT1-XS</strong> are now available in all AZ.
</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-ent1-xxs-and-ent1-xs-offers-available-in-the-ent1-range https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-ent1-xxs-and-ent1-xs-offers-available-in-the-ent1-range compute <![CDATA[Instances | PRO2 and PLAY2 Instances are now available in the AMS-1 Availability Zone]]> <p>The new generation of PRO2 General Purpose Instances are now available in all Availability Zones, including AMS1.</p> <p>The new generation of PLAY2 Development Instances is available in the PAR1, PAR2, AMS2, WAW1, WAW2 Availability Zones, and now also in AMS1.</p>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#pro2-and-play2-instances-are-now-available-in-the-ams-1-availability-zone https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#pro2-and-play2-instances-are-now-available-in-the-ams-1-availability-zone compute <![CDATA[Billing | The billing API is now available!]]> <p>With the billing API, you can now retrieve your current consumption and invoices via API.

This bug is now fixed.</p> <p>We also recommend you use the Serverless Functions dashboards in the <a href="/cockpit/quickstart/">Scaleway Cockpit</a> to monitor all your metrics and logs and access advanced features (ordering, filtering...)</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#missing-logs-in-console-and-api https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#missing-logs-in-console-and-api serverless <![CDATA[Transactional-email | The API documentation for Transactional Email is now live!]]> <ul> <li>The API documentation for Transactional Email is now live.
<a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/developers/api/transactional-email/">Check it out!</a></li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#the-api-documentation-for-transactional-email-is-now-live https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#the-api-documentation-for-transactional-email-is-now-live managed-services <![CDATA[Transactional-email | New attachment types are available for Transactional Email!]]> <ul> <li>Transactional Email now supports the following attachment types: calendar format (<code>text/ics</code>), (<code>application/ics</code>), and CSV files.</li> </ul>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-attachment-types-are-available-for-transactional-email https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-attachment-types-are-available-for-transactional-email managed-services <![CDATA[Transactional-email | TEM]]> <ul> <li><p>The new Transactional Email sending domain <code>scw-tem.cloud</code> is now available.
If you are a new client, you can use this sending domain by default.
If you were using the previous sending domain, you can <a href="/transactional-email/how-to/authenticate-domain/#how-to-configure-spf-records">configure a new SPF record</a> with <code>scw-tem.cloud</code> and delete the old SPF record.</p> </li> <li><p>Emails returned with error code 452 (recipient&#39;s mailbox is full) will be deleted with no further attempts made to resend.</p> </li> <li><p>A minimum number of characters is now required in an email to avoid it going to spam.
The subject and the body of the email must respectively contain at least 6 and 10 characters.</p> </li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#tem https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#tem managed-services <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Kubernetes 1.20 is no longer supported on our Kubernetes products starting January 15th]]> <p>Kubernetes 1.20 is no longer supported on our <a href="/kubernetes/quickstart/">Kubernetes products</a> starting January 15th.
Clusters in this specific version will automatically be upgraded to 1.21 on February 15th, 2023.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#kubernetes-120-is-no-longer-supported-on-our-kubernetes-products-starting-january-15th https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#kubernetes-120-is-no-longer-supported-on-our-kubernetes-products-starting-january-15th containers <![CDATA[Instances | Cost-Optimized Instances based on ARM available in PAR2]]> <p>We are excited to introduce our latest Cost-Optimized Instances based on ARM in the Paris region (PAR2), blending cost-effectiveness with
Ampere Altra&#39;s AI-optimized performance.</p> <p>Key features include:</p> <ul> <li>AI-optimized with Ampere Altra processors</li> <li>Up to 2.8x more energy-efficient than traditional x86</li> <li>Configurations from 2-32 vCPUs, 8-128GB RAM</li> </ul> <p>Migrate to COP-ARM for enhanced performance and sustainability.
Find out more on our <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/cost-optimized-instances-based-on-arm/">COP-ARM Instances page</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cost-optimized-instances-based-on-arm-available-in-par2 https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cost-optimized-instances-based-on-arm-available-in-par2 compute <![CDATA[Secret-manager | Secrets can be set as ephemeral]]> <p>Ephemeral secrets are now available on the Secret Manager API.</p> <p>Upon creation, secrets can now be configured with a time-to-live or to expire after a single access through an ephemeral policy.
If applied on a secret, all its versions will inherit from the policy.</p> <p>Refer to the <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/developers/api/secret-manager">Secret Manager API documentation</a> for more information.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#secrets-can-be-set-as-ephemeral https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#secrets-can-be-set-as-ephemeral security-identity <![CDATA[Topics-and-events | SNS Topic creation available in the console]]> <p>You can now create and manage your SNS Topics directly in the console.
For more information, you can refer to our <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/topics-and-events/how-to/create-manage-topics/">How to create topics documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#sns-topic-creation-available-in-the-console https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#sns-topic-creation-available-in-the-console serverless <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Proxy Registry is now available!]]> <p>Kubernetes Kapsule now benefits from a proxy registry in all regions, caching required system images for new clusters and new nodes.
This addition eliminates rate limiting from the official container registries used to pull system images, and eases scaling large node pools in <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/kubernetes/reference-content/secure-cluster-with-private-network/#what-is-the-difference-between-controlled-isolation-and-full-isolation">full isolation</a> mode.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#proxy-registry-is-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#proxy-registry-is-now-available containers <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Allowing IPs for Kubernetes Kapsule and Kosmos clusters]]>
<p>Kubernetes Kapsule users can now restrict cluster access by specifying allowed IPs in CIDR notation.
The default configuration <code>0.0.0.0/0</code> allows connections from all IPs.<br><Message type="note"> Kosmos clusters require unrestricted access from Scaleway IP subnets.
This security enhancement reduces the risk of unauthorized access.
Find more information in the <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/kubernetes/how-to/manage-allowed-ips/">documentation</a>.
</Message></p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#allowing-ips-for-kubernetes-kapsule-and-kosmos-clusters https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#allowing-ips-for-kubernetes-kapsule-and-kosmos-clusters containers <![CDATA[Iam | Deprecation of the ResellerCustomerBillingManager permission set]]> <p>Since January 8th, 2025, the permission set <code>ResellerCustomerBillingManager</code> cannot be used in policies.
This permission set was only available for clients that consume Scaleway services through a reseller.</p> <p>For Organizations that used this permission set, the permission set was renamed from <code>ResellerCustomerBillingManager</code> to <code>BillingManager</code>.
Scripts that used the former name of the permission set, such as Terraform scripts, should be updated to use the new <code>BillingManager</code> name.
</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deprecation-of-the-resellercustomerbillingmanager-permission-set https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deprecation-of-the-resellercustomerbillingmanager-permission-set security-identity <![CDATA[Cockpit | Custom retention is now available for Cockpit data sources]]> <p>Since January 1st 2025, you can <a href="/cockpit/how-to/change-data-retention/">edit the retention duration</a> of all your logs, metrics and traces data sources, and apply a different retention period to each of them depending on your needs.
Extended retention was previously managed by Cockpit&#39;s premium plans which are now deprecated.
Increasing your data retention above the default values is free of charge <strong>during the beta phase only</strong>, and will become billable again in a couple of months.
</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#custom-retention-is-now-available-for-cockpit-data-sources https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#custom-retention-is-now-available-for-cockpit-data-sources observability <![CDATA[Generative-apis | New models support Llama 3.3]]> <p><a href="/generative-apis/reference-content/supported-models/#request-a-model">Llama 3.3 70B</a> is now available on Generative APIs.</p> <p>Llama 3.3 is a fine-tuned version of the Llama 3.1 70b model, which was designed to approach the performance of Llama 3.1 405B on some applications.</p>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-models-support-llama-33 https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-models-support-llama-33 ai-data <![CDATA[Containers | Serverless Containers console improvements]]> <ul> <li>Added easy access to the unique identifiers of resources in the Serverless Containers <strong>Overview</strong> tab</li> <li>Added direct access to documentation in the listing of Containers namespaces</li> <li>Improved error messages display on Containers</li> <li>Improved some descriptions</li> </ul>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-containers-console-improvements https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-containers-console-improvements serverless <![CDATA[Functions | Serverless Functions console improvements]]> <ul> <li>Added easy access to the unique identifiers of resources in the Serverless Functions <strong>Overview</strong> tab</li> <li>Added direct access to documentation in listing of Functions namespaces</li> <li>Improved error messages display on Functions</li> <li>Improved some descriptions</li> </ul>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-functions-console-improvements https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-functions-console-improvements serverless <![CDATA[Jobs | Serverless Jobs console improvements]]> <ul> <li>Added easy access to the unique identifier of resources in the <strong>Job definition</strong> panel</li> <li>Added direct access to documentation in Jobs listing</li> <li>Improved error message display for Job runs</li> <li>Improved some descriptions</li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-jobs-console-improvements
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-jobs-console-improvements serverless <![CDATA[Jobs | Cancel queued jobs]]> <p>Sometimes job runs in <strong>queued</strong> status could not be effectively canceled.
This issue is now fixed.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cancel-queued-jobs https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cancel-queued-jobs serverless <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Container Storage Interface (CSI) driver v.0.2 is no longer supported]]> <p>The Scaleway Block Volume Container Storage Interface (CSI) driver <strong>v0.2</strong> will reach its end of support on <strong>February 28, 2025</strong>.
</p> <p>To ensure continued compatibility, <strong>we recommend upgrading to the latest version (v0.3)</strong> as soon as possible.

Large 3 is a frontier open-weight multimodal model, optimized for text generation, agentic tasks and image analysis.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-supported-model-mistral-large-3 https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-supported-model-mistral-large-3 ai-data <![CDATA[Containers | Improved memory metric accuracy for sandbox v2]]> <p>We have implemented a fix to improve the accuracy of memory usage metrics for Serverless Containers services.

Data Warehouse for ClickHouse® deployment metrics and logs, thanks to its integration with Scaleway Cockpit.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cockpit-is-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cockpit-is-now-available data-and-analytics <![CDATA[Environmental-footprint | Discover the environmental impact of your Load Balancers!]]> <p>Our Environmental Footprint Calculator now supports Load Balancers!
Designed to go beyond simple accounting, it provides impact metrics from full product life-cycle covering both CO₂ emissions and water consumption.
This multi-criteria approach helps you understand, compare and reduce the footprint of your cloud infrastructure.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#discover-the-environmental-impact-of-your-load-balancers https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#discover-the-environmental-impact-of-your-load-balancers billing <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Kubernetes 1.35 released]]> <p>This release upgrades the Kubernetes control plane and node components to version 1.35, including updated networking, storage, and observability components.</p> <h4>Control plane &amp; add-ons</h4> <ul> <li>Calico:
<code>v3.31.3</code></li> <li>Cilium: <code>v1.18.5</code></li> <li>Metrics Server: <code>v0.8.0</code></li> <li>CoreDNS: <code>v1.13.2</code></li> <li>CSI components: <code>v0.3.5</code></li> <li>Cloud Controller Manager (CCM): <code>v0.35.0</code></li> </ul> <h4>Node changelog components</h4> <ul> <li>Linux kernel: <code>6.8.0-64-generic</code></li> <li>containerd: <code>2.2.1</code></li> <li>CNI plugins: <code>1.9.0</code></li> <li>runc: <code>1.4.0</code></li> </ul> <h4>Feature gates available in 1.35</h4> <p>The following feature gates are available and can be enabled as needed:</p> <ul>
<li><code>HPAScaleToZero</code></li> <li><code>CPUManagerPolicyAlphaOptions</code></li> </ul> <blockquote> <p>Set to true by default for compatibility purpose, will be removed in 1.37</p> </blockquote> <ul> <li><code>MutatingAdmissionPolicy</code></li> <li><code>ImageVolume</code></li> </ul> <p>For more information, refer to <a href="/kubernetes/reference-content/version-support-policy/">Scaleway&#39;s Kubernetes version support policy</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#kubernetes-135-released https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#kubernetes-135-released containers
<![CDATA[Cockpit | Cockpit Grafana users are now deprecated]]> <p>Cockpit Grafana is now only available through your IAM console credentials.
The legacy authentication method, using Project-scoped Grafana users, is no longer supported.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cockpit-grafana-users-are-now-deprecated https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cockpit-grafana-users-are-now-deprecated monitoring <![CDATA[Environmental-footprint | Annual Environmental Reports now available!]]> <p>You can now generate and download annual environmental footprint reports directly from the Scaleway console or via the API.
The report provides a consolidated overview of your infrastructure&#39;s carbon emissions and water usage for the previous calendar year.</p> <p>This feature provides quick access to a downloadable PDF summary, supports ESG and Corporate Sustainability Reporting Directive (CSRD) compliance by providing structured environmental data, and allows you to track your infrastructure&#39;s environmental performance over time.</p> <p>The report is available for all active projects and covers the full previous year.
Access it now in the <a href="https://console.scaleway.com/environmental-footprint/reports"><strong>Environmental Footprint</strong> section</a> of the console.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#annual-environmental-reports-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#annual-environmental-reports-now-available billing <![CDATA[Environmental-footprint | Visualize your impact with environmental dashboards]]> <p>You can now track the evolution of your sustainability metrics directly within the console.

No need to download PDF reports anymore to get a high-level view of your impact.</p> <p>Available now in the <a href="https://console.scaleway.com/environmental-footprint/footprint"><strong>Environmental Footprint</strong> tab</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#visualize-your-impact-with-environmental-dashboards https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#visualize-your-impact-with-environmental-dashboards billing <![CDATA[Cockpit | Cockpit data exporter is now available in Beta]]> <p>You can now export your Scaleway metrics and logs can natively and in real-time to different

default on all new GPU pools, automatically bringing required software on your GPU worker nodes.
</p> <p>Find out how to activate or configure the operator in the <a href="/kubernetes/how-to/use-nvidia-gpu-operator/">documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#a-new-kubernetes-operator-to-support-all-gpu-pools-is-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#a-new-kubernetes-operator-to-support-all-gpu-pools-is-available containers <![CDATA[Vpc | Virtual Private Cloud is now in General Availability]]> <p>VPC allows you to quickly and seamlessly secure all your application architecture&#39;s resources from external networks.

Simply add an SQS Trigger from the console and when a new message is sent to the queue, it will trigger your Containers on the <code>/</code> path.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#triggers-for-serverless-containers-in-beta https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#triggers-for-serverless-containers-in-beta serverless <![CDATA[Instances | Zenbleed incident response]]> <p>We have successfully mitigated the Zenbleed vulnerability affecting AMD processors across our DEV1, GP1, and VC Instance offerings.

<p>Bucket policies can now be created, viewed and edited from the <a href="https://console.scaleway.com/">Scaleway console</a>.

Functions.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#rust-179-runtime-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#rust-179-runtime-now-available serverless <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Mitigated OpenSSH Vulnerability (CVE-2006-5051)]]> <p>A security regression (<a href="https://ubuntu.com/security/CVE-2024-6387">CVE-2006-5051</a>) was discovered in OpenSSH’s server (sshd).</p> <p>To address this issue, Ubuntu 22.04 images used in the Kapsule Kubernetes service have been patched.
The patch will be automatically applied to all newly created nodes in managed clusters.
</p> <p>Additionally, the patch will be automatically applied to existing nodes.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#mitigated-openssh-vulnerability-cve-2006-5051 https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#mitigated-openssh-vulnerability-cve-2006-5051 containers <![CDATA[Transactional-email | Fixed email filtering bug]]> <p>Fixed a bug affecting email filtering in the Scaleway console.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#fixed-email-filtering-bug https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#fixed-email-filtering-bug managed-services <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Kubernetes
version 1.30 is now available!]]> <p>Scaleway managed Kubernetes products will support the latest minor version 1.30 Uwubernetes until September 2025.</p> <p>In addition to patch versions 1.30.1, 1.30.2, and 1.30.3, releases 1.29.2 to 1.29.6, 1.28.8 to 1.28.11, 1.27.11 to 1.27.15, and 1.26.14 to 1.26.15 are also available.</p> <p>Read our newest <a href="/kubernetes/reference-content/version-support-policy/">version support policy</a> for more details on the 14-month support timeline.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#kubernetes-version-130-is-now-available

You can now verify your domains again.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#verify-domain-button-issue-fixed https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#verify-domain-button-issue-fixed managed-services <![CDATA[Generative-apis | Models now support longer and better conversations]]> <ul> <li>All models on catalog now support conversations to their full context window (e.g Mixtral-8x7b up to 32K tokens, Llama3 up to 8k tokens).</li> <li>Llama3 70B is now available in FP8 quantization, INT8 is deprecated.</li> <li>Llama3 8b is now available in FP8 quantization, BF16 remains
default.</li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#models-now-support-longer-and-better-conversations https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#models-now-support-longer-and-better-conversations ai-data <![CDATA[Ipam | IPAM (IP Address Manager) is available in Public Beta]]> <p>IPAM acts as a single source of truth for the IP addresses of Scaleway resources, offering a variety of functionalities to help you manage your Scaleway IPs efficiently.
Whether you are tracking all private VPC IPs or organizing your IP address space, IPAM is designed to simplify your workflow and enhance your productivity.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#ipam-ip-address-manager-is-available-in-public-beta https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#ipam-ip-address-manager-is-available-in-public-beta network <![CDATA[Secret-manager | Secret types are available in the Scaleway console]]> <p>Secret types are now displayed in the Scaleway console for better usability of secrets.</p> <p>All secrets not explicitly tagged appear as opaque, but you
can now decide to create new secrets of a specific <a href="/secret-manager/concepts/#secret-types">type</a> (among opaque, username/password, database credentials, SSH key, SSL/TLS certificate and key/value), and secrets versions will be of the same type.</p> <p>Read <a href="/secret-manager/api-cli/create-secret-with-type/">our documentation</a> for more details on how to create secret types using the Scaleway API.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#secret-types-are-available-in-the-scaleway-console

Account actions are tracked in the Paris region.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#account-management-actions-in-audit-trail https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#account-management-actions-in-audit-trail monitoring <![CDATA[Webhosting | 1‑Click WordPress now available on Web Hosting]]> <p>We are excited to announce the launch of 1‑Click WordPress Install on our Web Hosting offers!
</p> <p>You can now install a fully functional WordPress website in just one click, no technical knowledge required.
Whether you are building a blog, a portfolio, or an online store, getting started has never been easier.</p> <p>Available now for all Web Hosting plans via the <a href="https://console.scaleway.com/webhosting/">Scaleway console</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#1click-wordpress-now-available-on-web-hosting https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#1click-wordpress-now-available-on-web-hosting domains-and-webhosting <![CDATA[Mongodb | Managed MongoDB v1 API]]> <p>The Managed MongoDB API is now in <code>v1</code>.

Follow <a href="/managed-mongodb-databases/how-to/manage-users/">this documentation</a> to learn more about multi-user management.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#multi-user-management-on-managed-mongodb https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#multi-user-management-on-managed-mongodb databases <![CDATA[Partner-space | Cost manager is now available in the Partner Space]]> <p>Partners can now consult their clients&#39; consumption directly in the console, in a new Partner space section called “Billing”.</p> <p>They can filter by:</p> <ul> <li>Client (by customer Organization
name)</li> <li>Period (by year)</li> <li>Category (by product category, e.g.
Compute, Storage, Containers, with each category corresponding to a different color in the consumption chart and detailed list)</li> <li>Product (by product, with the consumption shown being the sum of all the products of the same type.)</li> </ul> <p>Refer to the <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/partner-space/how-to/track-client-consumption/">documentation page</a> for more details</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cost-manager-is-now-available-in-the-partner-space https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cost-manager-is-now-available-in-the-partner-space partners
<![CDATA[Webhosting | Removal of Redis from cPanel platforms]]> <p>As part of our ongoing effort to improve platform security, Redis has been uninstalled from the cPanel platforms.
This decision was made due to Redis being a high-privilege service that is not required for the majority of our users, and which may pose security risks if misconfigured or exposed.</p> <p>For Web Hosting users who rely on Redis, we recommend migrating to Scaleway Managed Database for Redis™.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#removal-of-redis-from-cpanel-platforms https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#removal-of-redis-from-cpanel-platforms domains-and-webhosting <![CDATA[Containers | Serverless Containers and VPC]]> <p>You can now connect your Serverless Containers to a
Private Network, allowing secure access between Serverless and resources inside the same Private Network, such as Managed Databases, Instances, and more.</p> <p><a href="/serverless-containers/how-to/use-private-networks/">Read more</a> about attaching a Private Network to a Serverless Container.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-containers-and-vpc https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-containers-and-vpc serverless <![CDATA[Functions | Serverless Functions and VPC]]> <p>You can now connect your Serverless Functions to a Private Network, allowing secure
access between Serverless and resources inside the same Private Network, such as Managed Databases, Instances, and more.</p> <p><a href="/serverless-functions/how-to/use-private-networks/">Read more</a> about attaching a Private Network to a Serverless Function.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-functions-and-vpc https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-functions-and-vpc serverless <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Release of newer Kubernetes patch versions]]> <p>The following Kubernetes patches are available:</p> <ul> <li><code>1.32.[4-7]</code></li>
<li><code>1.31.[8-11]</code></li> <li><code>1.30.[12-14]</code></li> </ul> <p>We have also added the <code>CPUManagerPolicyAlphaOptions</code> k8s feature gate, and enabled the <code>bpf-lb-external-clusterip</code> cilium option to easily use tools like <em>tailscale</em> or <em>telepresence</em>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#release-of-newer-kubernetes-patch-versions https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#release-of-newer-kubernetes-patch-versions containers <![CDATA[Key-manager | Scheduled deletion of Key Manager keys is available]]> <p>You now have access to a
dedicated <strong>Scheduled for Deletion</strong> tab for your Key Manager keys in the <a href="https://console.scaleway.com/key-manager/">Scaleway console</a>.
This new tab provides a clear, centralized view of all your keys that are scheduled for deletion within the next seven days.
This gives you a crucial window to review and take action on keys before they are permanently deleted.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#scheduled-deletion-of-key-manager-keys-is-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#scheduled-deletion-of-key-manager-keys-is-available security-identity <![CDATA[Generative-apis | New supported model - Devstral Small]]> <p><a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/generative-apis/reference-content/supported-models/#chat-models">Devstral Small 2505</a> is now available in preview for Generative APIs.</p> <p>Devstral Small is a
fine-tuned version of Mistral Small 3.1, optimized to perform agentic coding tasks.
It can be plugged <a href="/generative-apis/reference-content/integrating-generative-apis-with-popular-tools/">into your favorite IDE</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-supported-model-devstral-small https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-supported-model-devstral-small ai-data <![CDATA[Clusters-for-kafka | Multi-AZ clusters are now available]]> <p>Multi-AZ is now available for Clusters for Apache Kafka®: deploy the nodes (brokers) of your clusters across 2 Availability Zones with automatic failover and zero event loss.</p>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#multi-az-clusters-are-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#multi-az-clusters-are-now-available data-and-analytics <![CDATA[Opensearch | Upgrade your OpenSearch deployment]]> <p>You can now scale up your Cloud Essentials for OpenSearch deployment without downtime.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#upgrade-your-opensearch-deployment https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#upgrade-your-opensearch-deployment databases <![CDATA[Generative-apis | Deprecation - Serverless models]]> <p>Starting 1st July, 2026, Devstral
2, Voxtral Small, Gemma 3, Pixtral and Qwen 3 Coder on Generative APIs Serverless will be Deprecated and become End of Life in accordance with our <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/generative-apis/reference-content/model-lifecycle/">Model Lifecycle Policy</a>.

<li>Python 3.14</li> <li>PHP 8.5</li> <li>Rust 1.96</li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#functions-runtime-upgrades https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#functions-runtime-upgrades serverless <![CDATA[Elastic-metal | Proxmox Backup Server 4 available]]> <p>Proxmox Backup Server version 4, based on Debian 13 (Trixie), is now available across all Beryllium, Iridium, and Lithium offers, in multiple Availability Zones.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#proxmox-backup-server-4-available
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#proxmox-backup-server-4-available bare-metal <![CDATA[Audit-trail | Generative APIs events available in Audit Trail]]> <p>Generative APIs activity can now be tracked in Audit Trail.
Refer to <a href="/audit-trail/reference-content/adt-supported-endpoints/#generative-apis-supported-endpoints">our documentation</a> to see the list of available events.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#generative-apis-events-available-in-audit-trail https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#generative-apis-events-available-in-audit-trail monitoring <![CDATA[Containers | Serverless Containers v1beta1 deprecated]]> <p>Serverless Containers API <code>v1beta1</code> is now marked as <strong>deprecated</strong>, <code>v1</code> is the new reference version.</p> <p>For more
information, <a href="/serverless-containers/reference-content/v1-migration-guide/">read the dedicated documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-containers-v1beta1-deprecated https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-containers-v1beta1-deprecated serverless <![CDATA[Iam | IAM policies at the resource level for Secret Manager - Key Manager - IAM]]> <p>With new conditions in IAM rules using resource attributes, access can now be restricted on Secret Manager, Key Manager, and IAM itself.
For example, using these conditions, you can now grant access to:</p> <ul> <li>A secret or a secret folder in Secret Manager</li> <li>A key in Key Manager</li> <li>A user, SSH key, API key, application, policy, or group in IAM</li> </ul> <p><a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/iam/reference-content/understanding-resource-level-conditions/">For more information, read the dedicated documentation.</a></p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#iam-policies-at-the-resource-level-for-secret-manager-key-manager-iam
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#iam-policies-at-the-resource-level-for-secret-manager-key-manager-iam security-identity <![CDATA[Iam | IAM policy creation new interface]]> <p>The console interface for IAM policy creation has been changed, with new permission set categories and a more intuitive experience to select principals and scopes.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#iam-policy-creation-new-interface https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#iam-policy-creation-new-interface security-identity <![CDATA[Instances | Instance Templates]]> <p>We are pleased to
announce that Instance templates are now available.
</p> <p>An Instance template allows you to save a predefined, immutable server configuration, including Instance type, boot image, volumes, network settings, tags, and cloud-init scripts, so you can repeatedly deploy consistent Instances without manually re-entering parameters.</p> <p>You can create templates from scratch via the new Templates tab in the console, or save your configuration as a template during the standard Instance creation wizard.
</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#instance-templates https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#instance-templates compute <![CDATA[File-storage | File Storage is now in General Availability]]> <p><a href="https://www.scaleway.com/fr/file-storage">File Storage</a> is now in General Availability in the PAR region.</p> <ul> <li><p>For Kubernetes users, dynamically provision Persistent Volumes with ReadWriteMany access by referring to <a href="/kubernetes/how-to/use-sfs-with-kubernetes/">this documentation page</a></p> </li> <li><p>For non Kubernetes users, attach multiple CPU or
GPU instances to a shared and fully managed file system via the <a href="/file-storage/quickstart/">Console, CLI</a> or <a href="https://registry.terraform.io/providers/scaleway/scaleway/latest/docs/resources/file_filesystem">Terraform</a>.</p> </li> </ul> <p>To reach the ideal level of performance for your file system use cases, also refer to <a href="/file-storage/reference-content/performance-scaling/">performance scaling</a> and <a href="/file-storage/reference-content/file-system-instance-selection/">compatible instance type selection</a> recommendations.</p>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#file-storage-is-now-in-general-availability https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#file-storage-is-now-in-general-availability storage <![CDATA[Jobs | Serverless Jobs improved logs for Retries]]> <p>To improve the new Serverless Job <a href="/serverless-jobs/concepts/#retries">Retries</a> feature, we improved the logs visualization by adding the attempt number for each line of log.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-jobs-improved-logs-for-retries
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-jobs-improved-logs-for-retries serverless <![CDATA[Mongodb | MongoDB v8.0 now supported]]> <p>MongoDB v8.0 is now supported.
Follow <a href="/managed-mongodb-databases/how-to/mongodb-upgrade-engine-version/">this procedure</a> to upgrade your Database Instance(s).</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#mongodb-v80-now-supported https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#mongodb-v80-now-supported databases <![CDATA[Mongodb | Snapshot automation policy available via API]]> <p>Set up a snapshot automation policy on your Managed MongoDB instances by using the <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/developers/api/managed-database-mongodb/database-instances#update-a-mongodb-database-instance">update Instance API
endpoint</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#snapshot-automation-policy-available-via-api https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#snapshot-automation-policy-available-via-api databases <![CDATA[Opensearch | Expand your OpenSearch clusters on demand]]> <p>Adjust node count and disk size to match your growing requirements.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#expand-your-opensearch-clusters-on-demand https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#expand-your-opensearch-clusters-on-demand databases <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Public API changes]]>
<p><strong><u>Breaking</u></strong></p> <ul> <li>Removed parameter <code>replace</code> on <code>DeleteNode</code> route, the route <code>ReplaceNode</code> should be used instead.</li> </ul> <p><strong>Features</strong></p> <ul> <li><code>ReplaceNode</code> route is not deprecated anymore.</li> <li>Cloud-controller manager version is bumped to <code>0.36.2</code>.</li> </ul> <p><strong>Fixes</strong></p> <ul> <li>The values of the <code>min_size</code> and <code>max_size</code> fields are now pinned to <code>target_size</code> in the case of pools without <code>autoscaling</code>.</li>
<li><code>target_size</code> will always be within <code>min_size</code> - <code>max_size</code> range in the case of pools with <code>autoscaling</code>.</li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#public-api-changes https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#public-api-changes containers <![CDATA[Elastic-metal | Deprecation of legacy operating systems]]> <p>Effective October 1st, 2026, support for several operating systems will be officially deprecated because they have reached their End of Life (EOL).
</p> <p>The following are the affected OS versions and their EOL dates:</p> <ul> <li>Ubuntu 20.04: EOL May 2025</li> <li>Debian 10: EOL June 2024</li> <li>Debian 11: EOL August 2026</li> <li>Proxmox 7: EOL July 2024</li> <li>Proxmox 8: EOL August 2026</li> <li>CentOS 7: EOL June 2024</li> <li>Windows Server 2019: EOL September 2024</li> </ul> <p>Scaleway recommends upgrading your infrastructure to supported OS versions ahead of the deadline to ensure continued security and system stability.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deprecation-of-legacy-operating-systems
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deprecation-of-legacy-operating-systems bare-metal <![CDATA[Console | Webauthn based 1FA]]> <p>The Scaleway console now supports passkey (WebAuthn) authentication.
If your passkey supports user verification, it acts as a passwordless login method, combining both authentication factors into a single gesture.
Otherwise, it acts as a second factor alongside your password.</p> <p>Passkeys are currently available for account Owners only, not IAM Members.</p> <p>Secure your account from the <a href="https://console.scaleway.com/settings/account/security">Account Security</a> page in your <strong>Settings</strong> space.</p> <p>See the <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/account/how-to/use-2fa/#passkey-authentication">Passkey authentication</a> documentation for more information.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#webauthn-based-1fa
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#webauthn-based-1fa console <![CDATA[Cockpit | Cockpit Launch]]> <p>Cockpit is now available in General Availability.
Cockpit is a monitoring and alerting solution designed for multi-source observability.
Cockpit allows you to collect, store, and analyze metrics and logs from Scaleway, your applications, or any other cloud providers.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cockpit-launch https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cockpit-launch observability <![CDATA[Instances | New Production-Optimized and Workload-Optimized Instances available - POP2, POP2-HC, POP2-HM]]> <p>POP2, POP2-HC, and POP2-HM are our new reliable, high-performance Instances with dedicated vCPU for the most demanding workloads and mission-critical applications.
Choose the vCPU/RAM ratio that best matches your workload&#39;s specific requirements, whether it&#39;s general purpose (POP2), High CPU (POP2-HC), or High Memory (POP2-HM).
</p> <p>These new Instances are currently available in:</p> <ul> <li>fr-par1</li> <li>fr-par-2</li> <li>nl-ams-1</li> <li>nl-ams-2</li> <li>pl-waw-2</li> <li>fr-par-3 will follow in the coming weeks.</li> </ul> <p>Learn more about <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/production-optimized-instances/">POP2</a>, <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/workload-optimized-instances/">POP2-HC and POP2-HM</a> Instances.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-production-optimized-and-workload-optimized-instances-available-pop2-pop2-hc-pop2-hm
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-production-optimized-and-workload-optimized-instances-available-pop2-pop2-hc-pop2-hm compute <![CDATA[Load-balancers | SNI routes to HTTP backends are no longer supported]]> <p>Following our deprecation notice, it is no longer possible to create and use SNI routes with HTTP backends in Scaleway Load Balancer.

https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#bug-in-debian-bookworm-image-fixed https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#bug-in-debian-bookworm-image-fixed compute <![CDATA[Load-balancers | Backends now have the console experience they deserve]]> <p>We have completely redesigned the backend management experience in the console:</p> <ul> <li>The Load Balancer creation process has been streamlined</li> <li>The backend management pages have been completely reorganized to provide a clearer and more consistent experience</li> <li>You now have access to many backend configuration settings that

While API access was already available, you can now connect directly via SMTP servers.
Enhance your workflows with reliable and straightforward email delivery!</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-jobs-allows-smtp-traffic https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-jobs-allows-smtp-traffic serverless <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Kubernetes worker nodes with NAT IPs]]> <p>Starting this deprecation cycle, existing Kubernetes Kapsule or Kosmos worker nodes can be moved from NAT IPs to Routed IPs.
</p> <p>With Routed IPs, your worker nodes will receive a flexible IP address, maintaining IP consistency even if the Instance is restarted or the hypervisor is changed.</p> <p>NAT IPs will reach end of life on October 1st, 2024.

</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-containers-maximum-scale-raised-to-50-instances https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-containers-maximum-scale-raised-to-50-instances serverless <![CDATA[Elastic-metal | Elastic Metal now available in Warsaw]]> <p>Your favorite dedicated servers are now available in Poland, offered in both the <strong>WAW-2</strong> and <strong>WAW-3</strong> AZs 🌍.

already use DHCP attachment mode or static attachment mode with an IP address belonging to the Private Network subnet, you&#39;re not affected by this change.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#static-private-network-attachment-change https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#static-private-network-attachment-change network <![CDATA[Postgresql-and-mysql | PostgreSQL h3-pg extension is now supported]]> <p>The <a href="https://github.com/bytesandbrains/h3-pg">H3 PostgreSQL extension</a> enhances the PostGIS extension by providing bindings for <a

All members can now log in by using their account on Google, Microsoft or Github, similarly to Owner login.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#sso-for-members https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#sso-for-members security-identity <![CDATA[Iam | IAM Guest creation deprecation]]> <p>IAM Guest creation is no longer available.

Improve performance during wide operations such as sorting, grouping, and aggregations.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#custom-persistent-volumes-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#custom-persistent-volumes-available data-and-analytics <![CDATA[Environmental-footprint | Discover the environmental impact of your Instances now!]]> <p>Our Environmental Footprint Calculator now supports Instances!
Designed to go beyond simple accounting, it provides real usage-based impact metrics, covering both CO₂ emissions and water consumption.
This multi-criteria approach helps you understand, compare and reduce the footprint of your cloud infrastructure.
<a href="https://console.scaleway.com/environmental-footprint">Get my Impact report</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#discover-the-environmental-impact-of-your-instances-now https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#discover-the-environmental-impact-of-your-instances-now billing <![CDATA[Elastic-metal | Price drop for Ryzen EM-A610R-NVMe]]> <p>The Ryzen offer EM-A610R-NVMe now comes with a reduced price:</p> <ul> <li>Old price: €59.99/month</li> <li>New price: €39.99/month</li> </ul> <p>The offer is currently available in PAR-1 and PAR-2, and will soon be available in all
regions.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#price-drop-for-ryzen-em-a610r-nvme https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#price-drop-for-ryzen-em-a610r-nvme bare-metal <![CDATA[Secret-manager | Secret encryption with client-managed Key Manager keys]]> <p>Secrets can now be encrypted with any encryption key you may have stored in Key Manager.
You can choose to encrypt your payload using a symmetric key you manage, or continue relying on Scaleway-managed encryption, as before.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#secret-encryption-with-client-managed-key-manager-keys https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#secret-encryption-with-client-managed-key-manager-keys security-identity <![CDATA[Iam | Guest migration to IAM members]]> <p>In all Organizations, IAM administrators can decide to migrate Guests to Members to benefit from new security features.
The migration is performed by an admin and applies to all former guests of the Organizations.
</p> <p>Every new Member must log in using the Member login form and will receive an email to be informed about this change during the migration.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#guest-migration-to-iam-members https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#guest-migration-to-iam-members security-identity <![CDATA[Domains-and-dns | .be TLD now available]]> <p>Scaleway now supports<code>.be</code> domain name creation, renewal, and transfer.
This addition expands our support for European TLDs and offers more options for your online presence.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#be-tld-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#be-tld-now-available domains-and-webhosting <![CDATA[Generative-apis | Llama 3.3 70B maximum context update]]> <p>Llama 3.3 70B maximum context is <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/generative-apis/reference-content/supported-models/">now reduced to 100k tokens</a> (from 130k tokens previously).
This update will improve average throughput and time to first token.
<a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/managed-inference/reference-content/model-catalog/">Managed Inference</a> can still be used to support context lengths of 130k tokens.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#llama-33-70b-maximum-context-update https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#llama-33-70b-maximum-context-update ai-data <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Kubernetes Kapsule and Kosmos now support custom security groups]]> <p>Starting now, users can provide a security group ID for their existing security groups when creating a Kapsule pool.
If no security group ID is provided, the pool will use a default preconfigured security group named <em>Kapsule default security group</em>, which is shared among all Kapsule pools in the same zone.
The older behavior of creating a separate security group for each zone where the cluster had at least one pool has been discontinued.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#kubernetes-kapsule-and-kosmos-now-support-custom-security-groups https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#kubernetes-kapsule-and-kosmos-now-support-custom-security-groups containers <![CDATA[Kubernetes | End of support for Kubernetes versions 1.27, 1.28, and 1.29]]> <p>From April 7, 2025, clusters using Kubernetes versions 1.27, 1.28, and 1.29 will be automatically upgraded to Kubernetes version 1.30.</p>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#end-of-support-for-kubernetes-versions-127-128-and-129 https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#end-of-support-for-kubernetes-versions-127-128-and-129 containers <![CDATA[Dedibox | Start-9-M price drop, now at €39.99!]]> <p>We&#39;re excited to announce that our AMD Ryzen CPU-based offer, Start-9-M, is now available at a reduced price:</p> <ul> <li>Old price: €59.99/month</li> <li>New price: €39.99/month</li> </ul> <p>Our standard 12-month and 36-month commitments still apply, allowing you to benefit from an additional 5% or 15% discount on your
monthly fee, respectively.</p> <p>With this new pricing, our AMD Ryzen dedicated servers become the <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/amd-ryzen-dedicated-server/">market&#39;s most affordable 6-core dedicated servers</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#start-9-m-price-drop-now-at-euro3999 https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#start-9-m-price-drop-now-at-euro3999 bare-metal <![CDATA[Console | New Support Plans]]> <p>The new Scaleway Support Plans are now available.</p> <p>Our French Support team has been strengthened and trained to improve your service.</p> <p>In line
with this upgrade, we have refreshed our Support Plans with new pricing, clearer service levels, and expanded options.</p> <p>The new plans can be found in the <a href="https://console.scaleway.com/support/plans">Support Plans</a> page of the console.</p> <p>Find below an overview of the new plans:</p> <ul> <li><strong>Basic</strong> (Free) - 24/7 ticket support, 8h IRT for P1 incidents</li> <li><strong>Advanced</strong> (€50/month or 5% net spend*) - 2h IRT for P1s, Hotline 7/7 (9AM–6PM)</li> <li><strong>Business</strong> (€250/month or 10% net spend*) - &lt; 30min IRT for P1s, 24/7 Hotline,

<![CDATA[Billing | FinOps API now available in Beta]]> <p>The Scaleway FinOps API is now available in Beta.
This release allows analysis of costs with a per-resource granularity and fine-grained time granularity, and it also lays the foundation for upcoming evolutions in FinOps.</p> <p>For details, see <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/developers/api/billing_finops">the dedicated FinOps API Reference documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#finops-api-now-available-in-beta https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#finops-api-now-available-in-beta billing <![CDATA[Object-storage | SSE-KMS encryption is now available for Object Storage]]> <p>SSE-KMS provides server-side
encryption (SSE) of your objects using encryption keys managed through Scaleway&#39;s Key Management Service (KMS), giving you full control over your encryption keys while benefiting from a fully managed experience, keeping both data and encryption keys under European jurisdiction.</p> <p>Simply enable SSE-KMS at bucket creation, or from the settings of an existing bucket, and select the KMS key to use for encryption.
All objects uploaded after activating SSE-KMS will then be encrypted transparently using the configured key.</p> <p>Refer to our <a href="/object-storage/how-to/enable-sse-kms/">dedicated documentation</a> for more information.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#sse-kms-encryption-is-now-available-for-object-storage https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#sse-kms-encryption-is-now-available-for-object-storage storage <![CDATA[Vpc | Network ACLs are now in General Availability]]> <p>Network ACLs allow you to define fine-grained access control policies for your network

It was previously limited to 1,000 characters.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#environment-variables-limitation-upgrade-for-serverless-functions https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#environment-variables-limitation-upgrade-for-serverless-functions serverless <![CDATA[Audit-trail | Serverless SQL events available in Audit Trail]]> <p>Serverless SQL activity can now be tracked in Audit Trail.
Refer to <a href="/audit-trail/reference-content/adt-supported-endpoints/#serverless-sql-databases-supported-endpoints">our documentation</a> to see the list of available events.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-sql-events-available-in-audit-trail https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-sql-events-available-in-audit-trail monitoring <![CDATA[Jobs | Referencing a secret with a custom encryption key]]> <p>Referencing a secret from Secret Manager that uses a custom encryption key in a job definition caused job runs to fail during the validation step.
This issue is now fixed.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#referencing-a-secret-with-a-custom-encryption-key https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#referencing-a-secret-with-a-custom-encryption-key serverless <![CDATA[Jobs | Automatic retries for Serverless Jobs]]> <p>You can now configure automatic retries for Scaleway Serverless Jobs.
If a job fails due to transient errors or unexpected crashes, the system will automatically re-run it based on your defined policy, improving the resilience of your automated workflows without requiring manual intervention.</p> <ul> <li><strong>Customizable attempts:</strong> Specify the maximum number of retry attempts.</li> <li><strong>Enhanced reliability:</strong> Better handling of intermittent failures or third-party API issues.</li> </ul> <p>For more information, <a href="/serverless-jobs/how-to/manage-job-retries/">read the dedicated documentation</a>.</p>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#automatic-retries-for-serverless-jobs https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#automatic-retries-for-serverless-jobs serverless <![CDATA[Jobs | Multiple cron triggers for Serverless Jobs]]> <p>We upgraded how cron works for Scaleway Serverless Jobs, shifting from a single schedule to a powerful, multi-trigger model.</p> <ul> <li><strong>Multiple cron triggers:</strong> You can now attach multiple distinct cron schedules to a single Serverless Job, allowing for complex, multi-timeframe automation.</li> <li><strong>Dynamic overrides:</strong> Each
trigger can independently override the job&#39;s default startup command and arguments.
This gives you the flexibility to run the same codebase but process different tasks, environments, or parameters depending on when or how the job is triggered.</li> <li><strong>Schedule field deprecation</strong>: Current CRON schedules will continue to be executed but the field is marked as deprecated to encourage the usage of triggers.</li> </ul> <p>For more information, <a href="/serverless-jobs/how-to/manage-job-triggers/">read the dedicated documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#multiple-cron-triggers-for-serverless-jobs
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#multiple-cron-triggers-for-serverless-jobs serverless <![CDATA[Object-storage | TLS enforcement via bucket policies is now available]]> <p>Object Storage now supports the <code>aws:SecureTransport</code> policy condition key, allowing you to enforce the use of TLS for all requests made to your buckets and objects.
This enables you to deny any unencrypted (HTTP) access and ensure that data is always transmitted securely.
</p> <p>Simply add a bucket policy using the <code>aws:SecureTransport</code> condition to restrict access to requests made over HTTPS.</p> <p>Refer to <a href="/object-storage/api-cli/bucket-policy/">our dedicated documentation</a> for more information.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#tls-enforcement-via-bucket-policies-is-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#tls-enforcement-via-bucket-policies-is-now-available storage <![CDATA[Vpc-peering | VPC Peering is now in General Availability]]> <p>Scaleway VPC Peering is now available in General Availability in

controller at cluster creation is no longer possible]]> <p>The option to deploy an ingress controller in a Kubernetes cluster at cluster creation is no longer possible.
Users can still <a href="/kubernetes/how-to/deploy-ingress-controller/">deploy a pre-configured Ingress Controller</a> after cluster creation using the Easy Deploy feature within the <a href="https://console.scaleway.com/">Scaleway console</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deploying-an-ingress-controller-at-cluster-creation-is-no-longer-possible https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deploying-an-ingress-controller-at-cluster-creation-is-no-longer-possible containers <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Kubernetes 1.19 is no longer supported on our Kubernetes products starting October
15th]]> <p>Kubernetes 1.19 is no longer supported on our <a href="/kubernetes/quickstart/">Kubernetes products</a> starting October 15th.
Clusters in this specific version will automatically be upgraded to 1.20 at that date.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#kubernetes-119-is-no-longer-supported-on-our-kubernetes-products-starting-october-15th https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#kubernetes-119-is-no-longer-supported-on-our-kubernetes-products-starting-october-15th containers <![CDATA[Load-balancers | Load Balancers now support TLS on the backend!]]> <p>Load Balancers now let you explicitly configure encryption at the backend.
You can enable TLS for connections to servers in a given backend and select the port number without being restricted to 443.
The new &quot;Verify Certificate&quot; setting lets you specify whether the Load Balancer should check the server&#39;s certificate before establishing the connection.<br>You can use these settings to configure SSL bridging, having the Load Balancer terminate encrypted connections at the frontend, decrypt traffic, and re-encrypt it when forwarding it to backend servers.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#load-balancers-now-support-tls-on-the-backend https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#load-balancers-now-support-tls-on-the-backend network <![CDATA[Load-balancers | Load

https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#scaleway-transactional-email-is-now-in-public-beta https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#scaleway-transactional-email-is-now-in-public-beta managed-services <![CDATA[Cockpit | Devtools for Cockpit are now in production]]> <p>Devtools are now in production for Observability&#39;s Cockpit.
The following tools are available: </p> <ul> <li>SDK:<ul> <li>Cockpit</li> <li>Token</li> <li>Grafana User</li> <li>Alerts</li> </ul> </li> <li>CLI:<ul> <li>Cockpit</li> <li>Token</li> <li>Grafana User</li> </ul> </li> <li>Terraform:<ul> <li>Cockpit</li> <li>Token</li> <li>Grafana User</li> </ul> </li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#devtools-for-cockpit-are-now-in-production https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#devtools-for-cockpit-are-now-in-production observability <![CDATA[Secret-manager | Secret Manager is available in Public Beta]]> <p>Scaleway’s Secret Manager
is a service that allows you to store and manage sensitive information, such as API keys, usernames, passwords, and security certificates in a secure and centralized location.</p> <p>Secret Manager helps you manage the lifecycle of sensitive information:</p> <p>Secrets are encrypted and stored in a central and secure way, while data access is secured by an authenticated API call to avoid hard-coded transmission and protect your Organization against security breaches.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#secret-manager-is-available-in-public-beta
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#secret-manager-is-available-in-public-beta security-identity <![CDATA[Load-balancers | Host header routes are now available!]]> <p>Scaleway Load Balancers now provide the ability to create routes based on the value of incoming requests&#39; Host HTTP headers.</p> <p>Routes are used when a Load Balancer has multiple backends, to choose which of them to forward traffic to.
There are now two types of routes:</p> <ul> <li><strong>SNI routes</strong>: the only type we previously supported.
They use the SNI value provided during the TLS handshake.
This route type is suitable for TCP backends when TLS is enabled on the Load Balancer.</li> <li><strong>Host routes</strong>: this new route type uses the value of the Host header in the incoming HTTP request.
It is perfect for HTTP backends when you need to host several services behind your Load Balancer.</li> </ul> <p>The console user interface manages everything for you and configures the route type automatically based on the frontend and backend you use.
The API still lets you configure the route type but make sure you update your automation tools, as from June 1st we won&#39;t support the use of SNI routes with HTTP backends anymore.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#host-header-routes-are-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#host-header-routes-are-now-available network <![CDATA[Object-storage | Slowness issues fixed in Paris Region]]> <p>Some customers experienced slowness issues in <code>fr-par</code> over the last few weeks.
The problem is now resolved and latency has been divided by 3 starting Feb, 24th around 4:30 PM.
We continue to work on further updates to provide you with the best Object Storage performance.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#slowness-issues-fixed-in-paris-region https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#slowness-issues-fixed-in-paris-region storage <![CDATA[Functions | Serverless Offline Testing for Go and Python is available!]]> <p>Developing functions is now much easier with our local development framework for <a href="https://github.com/scaleway/serverless-functions-go">Go</a> and <a href="https://github.com/scaleway/serverless-functions-python">Python</a>.</p>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-offline-testing-for-go-and-python-is-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-offline-testing-for-go-and-python-is-available serverless <![CDATA[Transactional-email | Anti-spam policy for Transactional Email]]> <p>We have added an anti-spam policy for our customers to be able to use the Transactional Email (TEM) service.
<a href="https://tem.s3.fr-par.scw.cloud/antispam_policy.pdf">Find out about Transactional Email&#39;s anti spam policy</a></p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#anti-spam-policy-for-transactional-email https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#anti-spam-policy-for-transactional-email managed-services <![CDATA[Vpc | Regional Private Networks is available in Public Beta]]> <p>Regional Private Networks allow you to connect resources across multiple AZs within the same region and are currently only available in the PAR region.
</p> <p>Create a dedicated, private, and flexible Layer 2 networks to which you can attach Scaleway resources such as Instances, Load Balancers, Public Gateways, Elastic Metal, and Managed Databases.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#regional-private-networks-is-available-in-public-beta https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#regional-private-networks-is-available-in-public-beta network <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Scaleway Kubernetes products no longer support Kubernetes 1.23]]> <p>Clusters using the Kubernetes 1.23 version will be automatically upgraded to the Kubernetes 1.24
version after March 15th, 2024.

You can now use contextual parameters, such as concurrent executions, environment variables, and startup commands to customize a single job run, without having to duplicate the job definition.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-jobs-contextual-parameters https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-jobs-contextual-parameters serverless <![CDATA[Gpu-instances | New GPU L4 Instances]]> <p>We are excited to introduce our latest versatile and cost-effective GPU Instances based on <a href="https://www.nvidia.com/en-us/data-center/l4/">Nvidia L4</a> in the Paris
(PAR2) and Warsaw (WAW2) regions.</p> <p>Key features include:</p> <ul> <li>Nvidia L4 24 GB (Ada Lovelace architecture)</li> <li>4th generation Tensor cores</li> <li>4th generation RT cores (graphics capability)</li> <li>Available in multi-GPU 1, 2, 4 or 8 GPUs</li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-gpu-l4-instances https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-gpu-l4-instances compute <![CDATA[Gpu-instances | H100 PCIe GPU Instances now available in Poland]]> <p>We are excited to expand the availability of our H100 Instances, to provide you with the most high-end AI chip
of the market!
Scaleway&#39;s H100 Instances based on Nvidia H100 Tensor Core GPUs, are now available in the Warsaw (WAW2) region.</p> <p>Key features include:</p> <ul> <li>Nvidia H100 80GB (Hopper architecture)</li> <li>4th generation Tensor cores</li> <li>Multi-Instance GPU (MIG)</li> <li>Transformer Engine</li> <li>Available in multi-GPU 1 or 2 GPUs</li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#h100-pcie-gpu-instances-now-available-in-poland https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#h100-pcie-gpu-instances-now-available-in-poland compute <![CDATA[Jobs | Serverless Jobs fix usage of cached

Refer to <a href="/audit-trail/reference-content/adt-supported-endpoints/#audit-trail">our documentation</a> to see the list of events available.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#audit-trail-events-available-in-audit-trail https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#audit-trail-events-available-in-audit-trail monitoring <![CDATA[Block-storage | Volume capacity increased to 15 TB]]> <p>You can now provision individual Block Storage volumes with up to 15 TB of capacity, up from the previous limit of 10 TB.</p> <p>This update is designed to support data-intensive workloads
requiring increased vertical scaling.</p> <p>This increase applies to all new and existing 5K and 15K Block Storage volumes across all available regions.
You can scale your existing volumes directly through the Scaleway console, via the API, or using the CLI.</p> <p>For more details on managing your storage, refer to our <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/block-storage/">Block Storage documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#volume-capacity-increased-to-15-tb https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#volume-capacity-increased-to-15-tb storage <![CDATA[Data-warehouse | Data Warehouse deployments can now be stopped]]> <p>It is now possible to stop Data Warehouse deployments.
When a deployment is stopped, you will no longer be billed for vCPU consumption - only for Object Storage.

You can now deploy Instances, Kapsule, Block Storage, Object Storage, VPC, Databases, KMS, Audit Trail, and more in MIL-1.

Scaleway console or the Dedibox console for all compatible hardware configurations.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#proxmox-ve-9-and-proxmox-bs-4-now-available-on-dedibox https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#proxmox-ve-9-and-proxmox-bs-4-now-available-on-dedibox bare-metal <![CDATA[Object-storage | Lifecycle rules now support non-current versions in the Paris region]]> <p>You can now transition and expire non-current versions of objects in the Scaleway console with automated lifecycle rules in every region.

minimum before transitioning objects to Glacier</li> <li>30 days minimum before transitioning objects to Standard One Zone</li> </ul> <p>Existing lifecycle rules below these limits will continue to apply, but we recommend updating existing rules to comply with these new durations for optimal lifecycle management.
