taxes</li> </ul> <p><strong>These updated prices will take effect starting June 17, 2025.</strong></p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#price-adjustments-for-domain-extensions https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#price-adjustments-for-domain-extensions network <![CDATA[Instances | Block Storage volume creation removed from the Instance API]]> <p>Since April 30th 2025, it has become impossible to <strong>CREATE</strong> new block volumes using the Instance API (or through associated developer tools).
Customers can only list, mount, unmount, or delete volumes through the Instance API.
Block volume creation and management must go through the Block Storage API.
Learn more about the advantages of migrating from the Instance API to the <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/developers/api/block-storage/">Block Storage API</a> for managing block volumes and snapshots.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#block-storage-volume-creation-removed-from-the-instance-api https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#block-storage-volume-creation-removed-from-the-instance-api compute <![CDATA[Jobs | Startup command update on console]]> <p>Fixed an issue where, in some scenarios, modifying a job definition&#39;s startup command would not save the changes,
preventing users from updating their job definitions successfully.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#startup-command-update-on-console https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#startup-command-update-on-console serverless <![CDATA[Containers | New CPU tier in console]]> <p>We have added the option to deploy a Serverless Container with 2000 mVCPU in the console, enhancing customization.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-cpu-tier-in-console https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-cpu-tier-in-console serverless <![CDATA[Edge-services | WAF is now
available in Console]]> <p>Scaleway Edge Services&#39; <a href="/edge-services/reference-content/understanding-waf/">Web Application Firewall (WAF)</a> is now available in the console, in Public Beta.</p> <p>WAF provides automated protection for web applications against common threats and vulnerabilities, including SQL injection, XSS, remote code execution, and more.
It is based on the OWASP Core Rule Set to detect and block malicious requests.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#waf-is-now-available-in-console https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#waf-is-now-available-in-console network <![CDATA[Generative-apis | Managed Inference is now in General Availability]]> <p>Deploy latest AI models from our <a href="/managed-inference/reference-content/model-catalog/">model catalog</a> to benefit from guaranteed performance and strong security within your VPC.</p>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#managed-inference-is-now-in-general-availability https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#managed-inference-is-now-in-general-availability ai-data <![CDATA[Apple-silicon | M4-M and M4-XL now available!]]> <p>The Mac mini M4 lineup has expanded with two new models, available in PAR-1.</p> <p>M4-M, €0.29/hour.
It offers a more powerful alternative to the M4-S, with:</p> <ul> <li>10-core CPU</li> <li>10-core GPU</li> <li>32 GB RAM</li> <li>1 TB storage</li> <li>10 Gbps bandwidth</li> </ul> <p>M4-XL, €0.49/hour.
The most powerful Mac mini M4 Pro, with:</p> <ul> <li>14-core CPU</li> <li>20-core GPU</li> <li>64 GB RAM</li> <li>2 TB storage</li> <li>10 Gbps bandwidth</li> </ul> <p>For more details and to get started, visit Scaleway&#39;s <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/mac-mini-m4/">Mac mini M4 product page</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#m4-m-and-m4-xl-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#m4-m-and-m4-xl-now-available bare-metal <![CDATA[Containers | Tags for Serverless Containers]]> <p>Users can now assign tags to their <strong>Serverless
Containers</strong> and <strong>Namespaces</strong>, allowing for better organization, management, and filtering of resources within the Scaleway console and API.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#tags-for-serverless-containers https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#tags-for-serverless-containers serverless <![CDATA[Functions | Tags for Serverless Functions]]> <p>Users can now assign tags to their <strong>Serverless Functions</strong> and <strong>Namespaces</strong>, allowing for better organization, management, and filtering of resources within the Scaleway console and
API.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#tags-for-serverless-functions https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#tags-for-serverless-functions serverless <![CDATA[Containers | Serverless Containers healthcheck]]> <p>Fixed a bug with custom healthcheck where in specific conditions, Serverless Containers reported status was incorrect during a deployment.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-containers-healthcheck https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-containers-healthcheck serverless <![CDATA[Audit-trail | MongoDB events available in Audit
Trail]]> <p>MongoDB activity can now be tracked in Audit Trail.
Refer to <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/audit-trail/reference-content/adt-supported-endpoints/#mongodb-supported-endpoints">our documentation</a> to see the list of available events.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#mongodb-events-available-in-audit-trail https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#mongodb-events-available-in-audit-trail monitoring <![CDATA[Functions | Serverless Functions VPC routing available]]> <p><strong>VPC Routing</strong> is now fully functional for Serverless Functions.
While attaching to a Private Network was previously possible, traffic can now flow seamlessly between your functions and other private resources within your VPC.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-functions-vpc-routing-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-functions-vpc-routing-available serverless <![CDATA[Cockpit | Cockpit data exporter is now in General Availability]]> <p>You can now export your Scaleway metrics and logs natively and in real-time to different destinations, including Datadog and any OpenTelemetry-compatible endpoint.</p>
<p>Data exports can now be managed from the Cockpit menu in the console for easier access.
Check the <a href="/cockpit/how-to/manage-data-exports/">data exporter documentation</a> if you need any help.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cockpit-data-exporter-is-now-in-general-availability https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cockpit-data-exporter-is-now-in-general-availability monitoring <![CDATA[Elastic-metal | Ubuntu 26.04 LTS available]]> <p>Ubuntu 26.04 LTS (Resolute Raccoon) is now available across all compatible Elastic Metal offers.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#ubuntu-2604-lts-available
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#ubuntu-2604-lts-available bare-metal <![CDATA[Object-storage | Versioning and Object Lock are now fully supported in the Scaleway console]]> <p>Versioning lets you prevent accidental object alteration, preserve object history, search for specific object versions, and manage delete markers directly from the Scaleway console.</p> <p>Object Lock protects sensitive assets against ransomware and unauthorized changes through a guided interface that reduces the risk of misconfiguration.</p> <p>Refer to our dedicated <a
href="/object-storage/how-to/use-bucket-versioning/">versioning</a> and <a href="/object-storage/how-to/use-object-lock/">object lock</a> documentation pages to start protecting your data.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#versioning-and-object-lock-are-now-fully-supported-in-the-scaleway-console https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#versioning-and-object-lock-are-now-fully-supported-in-the-scaleway-console storage <![CDATA[Object-storage | Conditional writes and Terraform state locking are now supported]]> <p>Conditional writes improve data consistency by allowing
applications to create or update objects only when specific conditions are met, helping prevent accidental overwrites in concurrent environments.</p> <p>Terraform state locking enables safe collaboration by preventing simultaneous updates to the same remote state file when using Scaleway Object Storage as a Terraform backend.</p> <p>Refer to our <a href="/object-storage/api-cli/object-operations/">dedicated documentation page</a> to check supported conditional operations.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#conditional-writes-and-terraform-state-locking-are-now-supported
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#conditional-writes-and-terraform-state-locking-are-now-supported storage <![CDATA[Dedibox | Ubuntu 26.04 LTS is now available]]> <p>You can now deploy Ubuntu 26.04 LTS (Resolute Raccoon) on compatible Dedibox dedicated servers.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#ubuntu-2604-lts-is-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#ubuntu-2604-lts-is-now-available bare-metal <![CDATA[Generative-apis | New supported models - Gemma 4 - Qwen3.6 - Mistral Medium 3.5]]> <p><a
href="/generative-apis/reference-content/supported-models/">Gemma-4-26b-a4b, Qwen3.6-35b-a3b, and Mistral-medium-3.5-128b </a> are now available on Serverless and Dedicated Deployments.
All models are frontier reasoning models with strong coding and vision capabilities.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-supported-models-gemma-4-qwen36-mistral-medium-35 https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-supported-models-gemma-4-qwen36-mistral-medium-35 ai-data <![CDATA[Load-balancers | Load Balancers now support HTTP/2 and HTTP/3!]]> <p>HTTP/2 offers significant improvements on HTTP/1.1 in terms of both performance and security.
HTTP/2 is now supported both at the frontend and the backend of Scaleway Load Balancers when TLS is enabled.
No configuration is required to start using it.
HTTP/2 is now enabled by default in all HTTP frontends, and backends with TLS configured and will automatically be used when supported by the client or backend server.</p> <p>HTTP/3 is the next generation of HTTP protocol, built on top of QUIC.
HTTP/3 represents a big step forward for internet performance and security.
As this is a very new protocol, you have to enable HTTP/3 support if you wish to use it with a Scaleway Load Balancer.
HTTP/3 is currently only supported at the frontend.</p> <p><a href="/load-balancer/reference-content/http2-http3/">Find out how to set up your Load Balancers for HTTP/2 and HTTP/3.</a></p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#load-balancers-now-support-http2-and-http3 https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#load-balancers-now-support-http2-and-http3 network <![CDATA[Vpc | SMTP ports on VPC Public Gateway can be enabled]]> <p>Enable SMTP traffic on VPC Public Gateway to allow sending emails from resources located inside your VPC (ports <code>25</code>, <code>465</code>,
<code>587</code>, and <code>2525</code>).</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#smtp-ports-on-vpc-public-gateway-can-be-enabled https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#smtp-ports-on-vpc-public-gateway-can-be-enabled network <![CDATA[Public-gateways | SSH Bastion is now available on Public Gateway]]> <p>The <a href="/public-gateways/how-to/use-ssh-bastion/">SSH Bastion</a> feature helps you control and secure all your SSH keys for developer and sysadmin access.
Moreover, it will help to reduce the attack surface of any malicious actors.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#ssh-bastion-is-now-available-on-public-gateway https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#ssh-bastion-is-now-available-on-public-gateway network <![CDATA[Domains-and-dns | Domains and DNS is now in Public Beta!]]> <p><a href="/domains-and-dns/quickstart/">Domains and DNS</a> is a service that allows you to <a href="/domains-and-dns/how-to/register-internal-domain/">register</a> and <a href="/domains-and-dns/how-to/manage-external-domain/">manage your domains</a>
directly from the <a href="https://console.scaleway.com/">Scaleway console</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#domains-and-dns-is-now-in-public-beta https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#domains-and-dns-is-now-in-public-beta network <![CDATA[Cockpit | Traces are now available in beta]]> <p>Along with metrics and logs, you can now push and pull traces from your Cockpit.
Gain real-time insights and trace the heartbeat of your system with our new traces feature, based on Grafana Tempo.</p> <p>Read <a href="/cockpit/how-to/activate-push-traces/">our documentation</a> to find out how to start using our traces feature.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#traces-are-now-available-in-beta https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#traces-are-now-available-in-beta observability <![CDATA[Transactional-email | Transactional Email now supports the SMTP login method]]> <p>Scaleway&#39;s Transactional Email now supports the SMTP <code>LOGIN</code>
authentication method.
This new feature allows our users to benefit from an additional authentication option to secure their SMTP communications.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#transactional-email-now-supports-the-smtp-login-method https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#transactional-email-now-supports-the-smtp-login-method managed-services <![CDATA[Apple-silicon | fr-par-1 is disabled in the API]]> <p>For Apple silicon M1 servers, requests to <code>/apple-silicon/v1alpha1/zones/fr-par-1/*</code> will no longer receive responses.
All requests should now go to the PAR-3 AZ, using <code>/apple-silicon/v1alpha1/zones/fr-par-3/*</code>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#fr-par-1-is-disabled-in-the-api https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#fr-par-1-is-disabled-in-the-api bare-metal <![CDATA[Object-storage | New bucket policy version allowing to set up granular access to resources is now in GA!]]> <p>With version 2023-04-17 of bucket policies, you can grant users and applications granular permissions to specific buckets and objects.
Check out our documentation to discover this new <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/object-storage/api-cli/bucket-policy/">bucket policy version</a> , and learn <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/object-storage/api-cli/combining-iam-and-object-storage/">how to combine IAM and bucket policies</a> to set up granular access to Object Storage!</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-bucket-policy-version-allowing-to-set-up-granular-access-to-resources-is-now-in-ga
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-bucket-policy-version-allowing-to-set-up-granular-access-to-resources-is-now-in-ga storage <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Bumped Linux kernel to 6.2.0-36]]> <p>Worker nodes running Kubernetes version 1.28.x have been upgraded to kernel version <code>6.2.0-36</code>.
This change will automatically apply to new nodes and will take effect on existing nodes upon their next reboot.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#bumped-linux-kernel-to-620-36 https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#bumped-linux-kernel-to-620-36 containers <![CDATA[Transactional-email | Domain reputation score]]> <p>We have recently introduced <a href="/transactional-email/concepts/#reputation-score">domain reputation score</a>.
Every domain within your Organization has a score, reflecting its overall health.
The higher the score, the better the delivery performance for your domain.
Refer to our documentation for <a href="/transactional-email/reference-content/understanding-tem-reputation-score/">guidelines on maintaining a favorable reputation score</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#domain-reputation-score https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#domain-reputation-score managed-services <![CDATA[Transactional-email | Email Flags]]> <p>Get more information on your email delivery issues with <a href="/transactional-email/concepts/#flags">flags</a>.
Whether it is a <a href="/transactional-email/concepts/#hard-bounce">hard bounce</a>, <a href="/transactional-email/concepts/#soft-bounce">soft bounce</a>, <a href="/transactional-email/concepts/#flags">mailbox full</a>, or other issues, these flags provide immediate insights into the reasons behind delivery failures.</p> <p>You can filter on these flags via the API and from the <strong>Email activity</strong> tab of your Transactional Email domain in the Scaleway console.</p> <p>For more information, refer to the <a href="/transactional-email/concepts/#flags">flags concept page</a> or the <a
href="/transactional-email/how-to/manage-email-activity/">documentation</a> on how to manage your email activity.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#email-flags https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#email-flags managed-services <![CDATA[Load-balancers | Load Balancer logs are now available in Cockpit]]> <p>Load Balancer now generates logs when the state of backend servers changes, or when your frontend TLS certificates expire.
These logs are sent to your Scaleway Cockpit and you can browse them via the dedicated <strong>LB Logs Overview</strong> dashboard.
They provide information not only on the changes in state of backend servers, but also the reasons for these changes.
With these logs, you will be equipped to properly troubleshoot backend server issues.</p> <p>Read more about Load Balancer logs in our <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/load-balancer/how-to/monitor-lb-cockpit/#how-to-view-and-understand-your-load-balancer-logs">documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#load-balancer-logs-are-now-available-in-cockpit https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#load-balancer-logs-are-now-available-in-cockpit network <![CDATA[Secret-manager | Secret Manager console pages have been updated]]> <p>In the Secret Manager section of the
<a href="https://console.scaleway.com">Scaleway console</a>, you can now select a <a href="/secret-manager/concepts/#region">region</a> to display secrets and folders inside this region.
Whenever you create a secret, you no longer need to specify the region, as it will be created in the region you are currently working on.</p> <p>Find out more about Secret Manager in our <a href="/secret-manager/quickstart/">dedicated documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#secret-manager-console-pages-have-been-updated https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#secret-manager-console-pages-have-been-updated security-identity <![CDATA[Iam | IAM audit logs]]> <p>Audit logs about <strong>IAM resources such as applications, users, API keys, groups and policies</strong>
are now available.
</p> <p>You can check who created, updated or deleted an IAM resource in the <strong>dedicated Logs tab on Console, or using the Scaleway CLI</strong>.</p> <p>For more information, refer to the <a href="/iam/how-to/view-logs/">IAM logs documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#iam-audit-logs https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#iam-audit-logs security-identity <![CDATA[Object-storage | DeleteObjects is now applicable to VersionID]]> <p>It is now possible to specify a <code>VersionId</code> to limit deletion to specific objects versions, or to versions other than
current version, for multiple objects at a time.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deleteobjects-is-now-applicable-to-versionid https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deleteobjects-is-now-applicable-to-versionid storage <![CDATA[Object-storage | DeleteObject and DeleteObjects latency significantly decreased in all regions.]]> <p>The <code>DeleteObject</code> and <code>DeleteObjects</code> method now go through Scaleway Object Storage’s new gateway, bringing a significant performance improvement in all regions.</p>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deleteobject-and-deleteobjects-latency-significantly-decreased-in-all-regions https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#deleteobject-and-deleteobjects-latency-significantly-decreased-in-all-regions storage <![CDATA[Postgresql-and-mysql | Latest versions are now available for Managed Databases]]> <p>Scaleway Managed Databases now supports PostgreSQL 15.5, 14.10, 13.13, 12.17 and FerretDB 1.14.
The MySQL stability has also been improved.</p> <p>For more information, refer to the <a href="https://www.postgresql.org/about/news/postgresql-161-155-1410-1313-1217-and-1122-released-2749/">PostgreSQL</a> and <a href="https://blog.ferretdb.io/ferretdb-releases-v1140/">FerretDB</a> release notes.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#latest-versions-are-now-available-for-managed-databases https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#latest-versions-are-now-available-for-managed-databases databases <![CDATA[Postgresql-and-mysql | PostgreSQL pgvector is now available!]]> <p>The
<code>pgvector</code> extension enables many AI use cases by turning your PostgreSQL instance into a vector database.
Features like storing embeddings and vector similarity search are particularly interesting.</p> <p>For more information, refer to the <a href="https://github.com/pgvector/pgvector">pgvector github project</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#postgresql-pgvector-is-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#postgresql-pgvector-is-now-available databases <![CDATA[Edge-services | Edge Services is now in General Availability]]> <p>Starting November 1st 2024, <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/edge-services/">Edge Services</a> is in General Availability for both
Object Storage and Load Balancer usage.
This means that the product is now ready for production environments, and Edge Services is no longer free to use.
Find out about the new <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/pricing/network/#edge-services">Edge Services pricing</a> for full details.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#edge-services-is-now-in-general-availability https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#edge-services-is-now-in-general-availability network <![CDATA[Containers | Serverless network metrics are evolving]]> <p>In the Serverless Containers metrics panel, we added access to network information on received and transmitted data.
Refer to the <a href="/serverless-containers/how-to/monitor-container/">dedicated documentation</a> for more information on how to monitor a container.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-network-metrics-are-evolving https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-network-metrics-are-evolving serverless <![CDATA[Functions | Serverless network metrics are evolving]]> <p>In the metrics panel for Serverless Functions, we added access to network information on received and transmitted data.
Refer to the <a href="/serverless-functions/how-to/monitor-function/">dedicated documentation</a> for more information on how to monitor a function.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-network-metrics-are-evolving https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-network-metrics-are-evolving serverless <![CDATA[Generative-apis | Generative APIs now available in Public Beta]]> <p>Generative APIs let you access popular AI models via API.
This new service is 100% hosted in Europe, providing low latency and full data privacy for AI-powered applications.

This integration reduces complexity while enhancing security by keeping your secrets out of your codebase.

This change allows for more precise error identification and improved tracking of response details.
See the <a href="/transactional-email/reference-content/webhook-events-payloads">documentation</a> for full details.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#webhook-event-update-to-email-error-field https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#webhook-event-update-to-email-error-field managed-services <![CDATA[Object-storage | SSE-C Encryption is now available across all Scaleway regions]]> <p>Server-Side Encryption with Customer-Provided Keys (SSE-C) is now available across all Scaleway regions for new and existing buckets.
SSE-C enhances data protection for sensitive or private data stored in Object Storage, allowing to easily encrypt data during upload for added security, while giving you full control over your encryption keys.
For more information <a href="/object-storage/api-cli/enable-sse-c/">read our dedicated documentation to get started with SSE-C on Object Storage</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#sse-c-encryption-is-now-available-across-all-scaleway-regions https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#sse-c-encryption-is-now-available-across-all-scaleway-regions storage <![CDATA[Edge-services | Edge Services redirects HTTP to HTTPS for Object Storage integration]]> <p>Starting November 13, 2024, Edge Services cache servers will change how they handle HTTP (<code>port 80</code>) requests.
Instead of serving content over HTTP, they will issue an <code>HTTP 302</code> redirect to HTTPS (<code>port 443</code>), keeping the rest of the URL unchanged.
This update applies only to Edge Services integrations with Object Storage.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#edge-services-redirects-http-to-https-for-object-storage-integration https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#edge-services-redirects-http-to-https-for-object-storage-integration network <![CDATA[Containers | DNS resolution rate limit for Serverless Containers]]> <p>Starting <strong>November 26, 2024</strong>, a rate limit of <strong>20 DNS queries per second</strong> per Container instance will be applied.
This limit only applies to queries that require a full DNS resolution and is enforced independently for each Container instance.</p> <p>This rate limit is set at a level that is well above typical usage, so most users should not be impacted.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#dns-resolution-rate-limit-for-serverless-containers https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#dns-resolution-rate-limit-for-serverless-containers serverless <![CDATA[Functions | DNS resolution rate limit for Serverless Functions]]> <p>Starting <strong>November 26, 2024</strong>, a rate limit of <strong>20
DNS queries per second</strong> per Function instance will be applied.
This limit only applies to queries that require a full DNS resolution and is enforced independently for each Function instance.</p> <p>This rate limit is set above typical usage levels, so most users should not experience any impact on their applications.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#dns-resolution-rate-limit-for-serverless-functions https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#dns-resolution-rate-limit-for-serverless-functions serverless <![CDATA[Containers | IPv6 for Containers]]> <p>Serverless Containers are now compatible with IPv6 environments.</p>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#ipv6-for-containers https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#ipv6-for-containers serverless <![CDATA[Functions | IPv6 for Functions]]> <p>Serverless Functions are now compatible with IPv6 environments.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#ipv6-for-functions https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#ipv6-for-functions serverless <![CDATA[Transactional-email | Integration with Zapier]]> <p>Transactional Email (TEM) is now integrated with the Zapier workflow-automation software.

This engine processes incoming mail as the primary protection layer and provides:</p> <ul> <li>improved spam detection</li> <li>enhanced analysis powered by machine learning</li> <li>greater overall accuracy</li> <li>stronger protection against malicious links and attachments</li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#enhanced-email-filtering-on-web-hosting-cpanel-plans https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#enhanced-email-filtering-on-web-hosting-cpanel-plans domains-and-webhosting <![CDATA[Webhosting | New Web Hosting plans now live across all regions]]> <p>New Web Hosting

This information is available when looking at the payment history of an invoice.</p> <p>These messages help you quickly understand why a payment failed and provide guidance on how to fix the issue and complete the payment.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#detailed-payment-error-messages https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#detailed-payment-error-messages billing <![CDATA[Containers | Serverless Containers - Authentication for private resources via IAM]]> <p>Serverless Containers uses a product-specific authentication method based on JWT to authenticate requests for
private endpoints.
It is now possible to use the new IAM policy <code>ContainersPrivateAccess</code>.</p> <p>Previous authentication method based on JWT will be deprecated gradually.

private endpoints.

has been fixed.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#the-cold-start-delay-on-grafana-dashboards-has-been-fixed https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#the-cold-start-delay-on-grafana-dashboards-has-been-fixed observability <![CDATA[Cockpit | Grafana as a Service v2 is now in production!]]> <p>A new version of our Grafana-as-a-Service platform is now available with increased performance.

href="/iot-hub/reference-content/certificate/">Certificate Authority update documentation</a>, the <code>migration.iot.fr-par.scw.cloud</code> endpoint is no longer valid.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-iot-platform-server-certificate-authority https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-iot-platform-server-certificate-authority managed-services <![CDATA[Gpu-instances | New H100 PCIe GPU Instance]]> <p>We are excited to introduce our latest GPU Instances based on Nvidia H100 Tensor Core GPU, the most high-end chip on the market, in the Paris (PAR2) region.</p> <p>Key
features include:</p> <ul> <li>Nvidia H100 80GB (Hopper architecture)</li> <li>4th generation Tensor cores</li> <li>Multi-Instance GPU (MIG)</li> <li>Transformer Engine</li> <li>Available in multi-gpu 1 or 2 GPUs</li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-h100-pcie-gpu-instance https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-h100-pcie-gpu-instance compute <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Cloud Controller Manager now supports Private Load Balancers]]> <p>Private Load Balancers let you balance internal traffic or make your internal services highly available without being exposed to the
internet.
</p> <p>Kubernetes users can now use the <code>scw-loadbalancer-private</code> annotation on their services.
Load Balancers are public if this annotation is unset or false.
Find the <a href="https://github.com/scaleway/scaleway-cloud-controller-manager/blob/master/docs/loadbalancer-annotations.md#servicebetakubernetesioscw-loadbalancer-private">Cloud Controller Manager documentation here</a></p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cloud-controller-manager-now-supports-private-load-balancers https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cloud-controller-manager-now-supports-private-load-balancers containers <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Disable public IPs of your node pools (full isolation)]]> <p>Kapsule clusters can now keep or remove public IPs for new node
pools!</p> <p>Two options:</p> <ul> <li><strong>default</strong> - controlled isolation: nodes get private endpoints and keep their public IPs, protected behind a security group (dropping inbound traffic)</li> <li><strong>full isolation</strong>: nodes have only private IPs (Public Gateway required, single egress IP)</li> </ul> <p>The option is immutable after pool creation.
Both isolation modes can be used in the same cluster.</p> <p>The feature is now available in Scaleway console (new node pools), Scaleway API, CLI and <a href="https://registry.terraform.io/providers/scaleway/scaleway/latest/docs/resources/k8s_pool#public_ip_disabled">Terraform</a> (public_ip_disabled)</p> <p>Find more details on our <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/kubernetes/reference-content/secure-cluster-with-private-network/">reference documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#disable-public-ips-of-your-node-pools-full-isolation
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#disable-public-ips-of-your-node-pools-full-isolation containers <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Kapsule clusters with only public IPs]]> <p>Hardening security is a top priority.
</p> <p>Effective now, every new Kapsule cluster is required to have a (free) Private Network attached.</p> <p>Starting this deprecation cycle, existing Kapsule clusters must be connected to a Private Network.
</p> <p>The default worker nodes&#39; isolation configuration will keep public IPs for nodes to access the Internet, and add private endpoints for resources to communicate securely.
Security groups aren&#39;t overridden at migration and RR wildcard DNS still point to public IPs.</p> <p>More information on the migration steps is provided in <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/kubernetes/reference-content/secure-cluster-with-private-network/">our reference documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#kapsule-clusters-with-only-public-ips https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#kapsule-clusters-with-only-public-ips containers <![CDATA[Containers | Trigger private containers from Messaging and Queuing (SQS/NATS)]]> <p>It is now possible to
create SQS and NATS triggers on private containers.</p> <Message type="note"> For now, changing the privacy of a container from public to private will require you to delete and recreate the existing triggers.

require you to delete and recreate the existing triggers.
</Message> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#trigger-private-functions-from-messaging-and-queuing-sqsnats https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#trigger-private-functions-from-messaging-and-queuing-sqsnats serverless <![CDATA[Object-storage | Our Amsterdam region is now multi-AZ by design for hot storage!]]> <p>The multi-AZ Standard class is now available in the Amsterdam region, ensuring the resiliency of your data across 3 Availability Zones (AZ) for all new objects uploaded in Standard class from now on.
This is our second region to support multi-AZ by design and to offer 99,9% of availability SLA, after the Paris region.
Check out our <a href="/object-storage/faq/">FAQ</a> for more information.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#our-amsterdam-region-is-now-multi-az-by-design-for-hot-storage https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#our-amsterdam-region-is-now-multi-az-by-design-for-hot-storage storage <![CDATA[Nats | Messaging and Queuing is now in General Availability]]> <p>Messaging and Queuing is Scaleway&#39;s message broker solution that allows you to send messages and trigger events between your cloud resources.</p> <p>The service supports 3 protocols: NATS, SQS and SNS (the latter is
still in Public Beta).
Messaging and Queuing is serverless-based, so you can run it without worrying about provisioning and you only pay for what you use.</p> <p>The Messaging and Queuing features are available via the Scaleway console, the CLI and Terraform.</p> <p>Your existing resources will need to be migrated, as communicated to all beta users.
</p> <p>If you have any questions, reach out to the team on the #messaging-queuing community Slack channel.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#messaging-and-queuing-is-now-in-general-availability https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#messaging-and-queuing-is-now-in-general-availability serverless <![CDATA[Queues | Messaging and Queuing is now in General Availability]]> <p>Messaging and Queuing is Scaleway&#39;s message broker solution that allows you to send messages and trigger events between your cloud resources.</p> <p>The service supports 3 protocols: NATS, SQS and SNS
(the latter is still in Public Beta).

</p> <p>If you have any questions, reach out to the team on the #messaging-queuing community Slack channel.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#messaging-and-queuing-is-now-in-general-availability https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#messaging-and-queuing-is-now-in-general-availability serverless <![CDATA[Topics-and-events | Messaging and Queuing is now in General Availability]]> <p>Messaging and Queuing is Scaleway&#39;s message broker solution that allows you to send messages and trigger events between your cloud resources.</p> <p>The service supports 3 protocols: NATS,

</p> <p>If you have any questions, reach out to the team on the #messaging-queuing community Slack channel.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#messaging-and-queuing-is-now-in-general-availability https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#messaging-and-queuing-is-now-in-general-availability serverless <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Support for Kubernetes Kapsule multi-AZ setup]]> <p>Enhance your cluster resilience by adding pools in multiple Availability Zones (AZs), and distributing workloads across at least two AZs.</p> <p>Scaleway&#39;s Console, API, CLI, and Terraform now all support
multi-AZ Kubernetes Kapsule clusters.</p> <p>Instructions are available in our <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/kubernetes/reference-content/multi-az-clusters/">reference documentation</a>.

href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/transactional-email/reference-content/tem-capabilities-and-limits/#types-of-attachments-available">supported file types documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-attachment-formats-are-now-supported https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-attachment-formats-are-now-supported domains-and-webhosting <![CDATA[Generative-apis | Devstral small is now deprecated]]> <p><a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/generative-apis/reference-content/supported-models/">Devstral small</a> will not exit Preview stage and is now
deprecated.</p> <p>Following our <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/generative-apis/reference-content/model-lifecycle/">model lifecycle policy</a>, this model will remain accessible until November 14th, 2025.
After this date, requests will be routed automatically to a similar model: <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/generative-apis/reference-content/supported-models/">Qwen3 Coder</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#devstral-small-is-now-deprecated https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#devstral-small-is-now-deprecated ai-data <![CDATA[Jobs | Serverless Jobs now enforce cross-product permissions]]> <p>When starting a Job definition with <code>ServerlessJobsFullAccess</code> permission:</p> <ul> <li>If the Job definition uses an image from <strong>Container
Registry</strong>, you must now add, at minimum, the <code>ContainerRegistryReadOnly</code> permission.</li> <li>If the Job Definition consumes data from <strong>Secret Manager</strong>; you must now add, at minimum, the <code>SecretManagerSecretAccess</code> permission.</li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-jobs-now-enforce-cross-product-permissions https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-jobs-now-enforce-cross-product-permissions serverless <![CDATA[Postgresql-and-mysql | VPC routing now compatible with Managed PostgreSQL and MySQL]]> <p>You can now
connect Managed PostgreSQL &amp; MySQL instances with resources located in other Private Networks from the same VPC.

reduced:</p> <table> <thead> <tr> <th align="left">Offer</th> <th align="left">Old Price</th> <th align="left">New Price</th> </tr> </thead> <tbody><tr> <td align="left"><strong>Start-2-S-SSD</strong></td> <td align="left">€7.99</td> <td align="left"><strong>€4.99</strong></td> </tr> <tr> <td align="left"><strong>Store-4-XL</strong></td> <td align="left">€399.99</td> <td align="left"><strong>€199.99</strong></td> </tr> <tr> <td align="left"><strong>Store-4-XXL</strong></td> <td align="left">€499.99</td> <td align="left"><strong>€359.99</strong></td> </tr> <tr> <td

change)</h3> <p>To provide you with more storage capacity at no extra cost, the SSD disk capacity has been doubled on the offers below:</p> <table> <thead> <tr> <th align="left">Offer</th> <th align="left">Old Storage</th> <th align="left">New Storage</th> </tr> </thead> <tbody><tr> <td align="left"><strong>Core-4-S-SSD</strong></td> <td align="left">3 x 500 GB SSD</td> <td align="left"><strong>3 x 960 GB SSD</strong></td> </tr> <tr> <td align="left"><strong>Core-4-M-SSD</strong></td> <td align="left">5 x 500 GB SSD</td> <td align="left"><strong>5 x 960 GB SSD</strong></td> </tr> <tr> <td
align="left"><strong>Core-4-L-SSD</strong></td> <td align="left">5 x 500 GB SSD</td> <td align="left"><strong>5 x 960 GB SSD</strong></td> </tr> </tbody></table> <p><em>Note: The other specifications (CPU, RAM) for these offers remain unchanged.</em></p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#dedibox-server-offer-updates https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#dedibox-server-offer-updates bare-metal <![CDATA[Audit-trail | Instances events available in Audit Trail]]> <p>Instances activity can now be tracked in Audit Trail.
Read <a href="/audit-trail/reference-content/adt-supported-endpoints/#instances">our documentation</a> to see the list of events available.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#instances-events-available-in-audit-trail https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#instances-events-available-in-audit-trail monitoring <![CDATA[Data-warehouse | Data Warehouse for ClickHouse® available in Public Beta!]]> <p>Our brand new Data Warehouse product is now available in Public Beta!
<a href="https://console.scaleway.com/data-warehouse/fr-par/deployments">Join the beta</a> and benefit from powerful analytics without the need to manage infrastructure.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#data-warehouse-for-clickhouser-available-in-public-beta https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#data-warehouse-for-clickhouser-available-in-public-beta data-and-analytics <![CDATA[Generative-apis | Whisper Large v3 is now available]]> <p><a href="/generative-apis/reference-content/supported-models/">Whisper Large v3</a> is now available on Generative APIs.</p> <p>Both Whisper
Large v3 and Voxtral Small 24B can now be queried to perform audio transcription using <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/developers/api/generative-apis/#path-audio-create-an-audio-transcription">Audio Transcriptions API</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#whisper-large-v3-is-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#whisper-large-v3-is-now-available ai-data <![CDATA[Domains-and-dns | New API endpoint - Failed Transfer Retry]]> <p>It is now possible to retry a failed domain transfer directly through the API without contacting support.
If a transfer fails, for example, due to an incorrect transfer code, you can relaunch it independently and at no cost.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-api-endpoint-failed-transfer-retry https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-api-endpoint-failed-transfer-retry domains-and-webhosting <![CDATA[Elastic-metal | Cloud-init feature available on Elastic-Metal servers]]> <p>Cloud-init is now available for Elastic Metal servers, enabling automated configuration at first boot.
You can now use user-data scripts to set hostnames, configure networking, install packages, and add SSH keys automatically, bringing cloud-like flexibility to bare metal provisioning.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cloud-init-feature-available-on-elastic-metal-servers https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cloud-init-feature-available-on-elastic-metal-servers bare-metal <![CDATA[Redistm | Support for Redis v7.2.11]]> <p>Redis v7.2.11 is now available.

tests]]> <p>Nightly tests are now run to perform sanity checks on the environments.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#nightly-e2e-tests https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#nightly-e2e-tests devtools <![CDATA[Packer | Optional step to clean up machine-specific data]]> <p>We have added an optional step to remove information related to the Instance used to build the image, such as its machine ID.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#optional-step-to-clean-up-machine-specific-data

Examples have also been added.

<p><code>VolumeServerTemplate</code> supports the creation of volumes from snapshots.
With this template type, you can also force a server to boot from the newly created volume.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#migrated-to-volumeservertemplate https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#migrated-to-volumeservertemplate devtools <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Kubernetes version 1.28 is now available!]]> <p>Scaleway managed Kubernetes products now support the latest minor version 1.28 Planternetes.</p> <p>Patch versions 1.27.3, 1.27.4, 1.26.6, 1.26.7, 1.25.11, 1.25.12, 1.24.15, 1.24.16 have also been released.</p> <p>Read <a
href="/kubernetes/reference-content/version-support-policy/">our version support policy</a> for more information.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#kubernetes-version-128-is-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#kubernetes-version-128-is-now-available containers <![CDATA[Kubernetes | New Availability Zone WAW-3 🇵🇱 supported by Kapsule and Kosmos]]> <p>To support our customers in the Central European region, Kapsule and Kosmos clusters can have node pools in our new data center located in Warsaw, Poland.</p>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-availability-zone-waw-3-supported-by-kapsule-and-kosmos https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-availability-zone-waw-3-supported-by-kapsule-and-kosmos containers <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Bumped images to Ubuntu 22.04 and 6.2 kernel]]> <p>Starting from Kubernetes version <code>1.28.0</code>, worker nodes are now using Ubuntu 22.04 and 6.2 kernel.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#bumped-images-to-ubuntu-2204-and-62-kernel https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#bumped-images-to-ubuntu-2204-and-62-kernel containers
<![CDATA[Object-storage | Migration to Hive backend is completed!]]> <p>All Regions are now fully migrated on the Hive backend.
This important milestone is the cornerstone of our performance plan for Object Storage and will be followed by the deployment of the new gateway.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#migration-to-hive-backend-is-completed https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#migration-to-hive-backend-is-completed storage <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Dedicated control plane offers are now available!]]> <p>Dedicated control planes are now generally available for Kapsule and Kosmos!
Boost your Kubernetes performance thanks to our dedicated control planes, available with 4, 8 or 16GB RAM.
An exclusive resource to orchestrate mission-critical, at scale and production workloads, highly available with a 99.5% uptime SLA!
</p> <p>Dedicated control planes are available in the <a href="https://console.scaleway.com/">Scaleway console</a>, the CLI, and Terraform.
Refer to <a href="/kubernetes/reference-content/kubernetes-control-plane-offers/">our documentation</a> for more information.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#dedicated-control-plane-offers-are-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#dedicated-control-plane-offers-are-now-available containers <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Kapsule and Kosmos clusters have a new limit on first offer.]]> <p>Starting September 1st 2023, Kapsule and Kosmos clusters are limited to 150 nodes maximum on first offer.
However, to go beyond 150 nodes, Scaleway offers a range of dedicated control planes to cater to a wide range of use-cases.
Refer to our <a href="/kubernetes/reference-content/kubernetes-control-plane-offers/">Kubernetes control plane offers overview</a> for more information.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#kapsule-and-kosmos-clusters-have-a-new-limit-on-first-offer https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#kapsule-and-kosmos-clusters-have-a-new-limit-on-first-offer containers <![CDATA[Load-balancers | Load Balancer is now available in AMS3 AZ]]> <p>We are happy to announce that the Scaleway Load Balancer service is now available in the new AMS3 (Amsterdam 3) Availability Zone, providing you with
more location options when building your service.
</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#load-balancer-is-now-available-in-ams3-az https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#load-balancer-is-now-available-in-ams3-az network <![CDATA[Instances | Stardust Learning Instances available in WAW-2]]> <p>We are excited to share that our Stardust Learning Instances are now available in the WAW2 (Warsaw 2) region!
This expansion provides you with even more options for deploying your Instances.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#stardust-learning-instances-available-in-waw-2 https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#stardust-learning-instances-available-in-waw-2 compute <![CDATA[Object-storage | Access logs now available in the Paris region]]> <p>Starting today, access logs are now available in Cockpit for the Paris region!
This enhancement will provide you with even greater visibility and control over your data.</p> <p>Access logs are invaluable tools that record detailed information about every interaction with your objects in the Object Storage service.
These logs include essential data such as who accessed your objects, when they accessed them, and from which IP address.
With access logs enabled, you can gain deeper insights into how your data is being used, and enhance your security and compliance efforts.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#access-logs-now-available-in-the-paris-region https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#access-logs-now-available-in-the-paris-region storage <![CDATA[Transactional-email | The Scaleway blackhole MX is now available!]]> <p>Scaleway has implemented a blackhole MX server you can use with Transactional Email, to prevent your emails from being rejected by recipient servers.
This happens when you have no MX record or when you have configured a null MX record.
<a href="/transactional-email/how-to/authenticate-domain/#how-to-configure-mx-records">Find out how to configure Scaleway&#39;s MX.</a></p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#the-scaleway-blackhole-mx-is-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#the-scaleway-blackhole-mx-is-now-available managed-services <![CDATA[Load-balancers | Private Load Balancers are now available!]]> <p>When creating a Load Balancer you can now choose whether you want it to be public (connected to the internet with a public IP address) or private (fully isolated from the internet and with no
public IP address).
Private Load Balancers let you balance internal traffic or make your internal services highly available without being exposed to the internet.
Make sure you attach your private Load Balancer to a Private Network so your internal clients can access it.
Read more about private Load Balancers in our <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/load-balancer/concepts/#private-load-balancer">documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#private-load-balancers-are-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#private-load-balancers-are-now-available network <![CDATA[Load-balancers | Static attachment is no longer available]]> <p>With the release of <a href="/vpc/concepts/#vpc">VPCs</a> and their managed <a href="/vpc/concepts/#dhcp">DHCP</a> feature, we are reworking the way resources are attached to a Private
Network, in order to offer a bullet-proof experience.
To this end, the <strong>static attachment mode</strong> that allowed for manual configuration of an IP address when attaching a Load Balancer to a Private Network from the Scaleway console, is no longer available.
This mode is not in line with the all-new Private Network management engine Scaleway is working on, and could result in non working configurations.
In removing static attachment mode, we are encouraging adoption of best practices that will future-proof your architectures.
Simultaneously, we are also working on a set of new features to manage your IP addressing space more granularly.</p> <p>While the feature will no longer be available from the Scaleway console, it is still available in the API, though entering a deprecation cycle.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#static-attachment-is-no-longer-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#static-attachment-is-no-longer-available network <![CDATA[Postgresql-and-mysql | Cross Availability Zone Read Replicas are now available!]]> <p> The Cross-AZ Read Replica feature ensures your data is
replicated in a dedicated read-only node, which is located in another AZ within the same Region as your main node.</p> <p> For more information, refer to the <a href="/managed-databases-for-postgresql-and-mysql/how-to/manage-read-replicas/">How to create Read Replicas</a> documentation page.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cross-availability-zone-read-replicas-are-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#cross-availability-zone-read-replicas-are-now-available databases <![CDATA[Nats | Messaging and Queuing metrics available in Cockpit]]> <p> Cockpit integration
is available for SQS, NATS &amp; SNS.
Refer to the documentation to find out how to monitor your Messaging and queuing metrics.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#messaging-and-queuing-metrics-available-in-cockpit https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#messaging-and-queuing-metrics-available-in-cockpit serverless <![CDATA[Queues | Messaging and Queuing metrics available in Cockpit]]> <p> Cockpit integration is available for SQS, NATS &amp; SNS.
Refer to the documentation to find out how to monitor your Messaging and queuing metrics.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#messaging-and-queuing-metrics-available-in-cockpit https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#messaging-and-queuing-metrics-available-in-cockpit serverless <![CDATA[Topics-and-events | Messaging and Queuing metrics available in Cockpit]]> <p> Cockpit integration is available for SQS, NATS &amp; SNS.
Refer to the documentation to find out how to monitor your Messaging and queuing metrics.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#messaging-and-queuing-metrics-available-in-cockpit https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#messaging-and-queuing-metrics-available-in-cockpit serverless <![CDATA[Secret-manager | Secret Path - Organize your secrets using folders]]> <p>You can now organize secrets into folders and subfolders for better management.
This allows you to efficiently categorize and locate your secrets based on applications, environments, or other criteria.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#secret-path-organize-your-secrets-using-folders https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#secret-path-organize-your-secrets-using-folders security-identity <![CDATA[Functions | You can now test functions from the Scaleway console!]]> <p>The new function Test feature allows you to run your code directly from the Scaleway console using a selection of methods, to make sure it behaves as expected.
For more information, refer to the <a href="/serverless-functions/how-to/test-a-function/">dedicated documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#you-can-now-test-functions-from-the-scaleway-console https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#you-can-now-test-functions-from-the-scaleway-console serverless <![CDATA[Functions | New function creation workflow available]]> <p>The new function creation workflow allows you to easily create a namespace and function in 2 clicks.
For more information, refer to the documentation on <a href="/serverless-functions/quickstart/#how-to-create-a-function">how to create a function</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-function-creation-workflow-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-function-creation-workflow-available serverless <![CDATA[Domains-and-dns | Pricing update for some TLDs]]> <p>Starting Monday 2nd September 2024, a new pricing will apply to the following TLDs when buying, renewing, and transferring domains: <code>.beauty</code>, <code>.college</code>, <code>.hair</code>,
<code>.makeup</code>, <code>.monster</code>, <code>.quest</code>, <code>.rent</code>, and <code>.skin</code>.</p> <p>From Monday 9th September 2024, the pricing for <code>.com</code> and <code>.net</code> TLDs will also change.</p> <p>Refer to the <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/domains-name/">dedicated page</a> to consult our new pricing.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#pricing-update-for-some-tlds https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#pricing-update-for-some-tlds network <![CDATA[Kubernetes | New size limits for ETCD stores in Kubernetes control planes]]> <p>To
maintain optimal performance of your managed Scaleway Kubernetes clusters, etcd stores in control planes are now subject to size limits: 55 MB for mutualized control planes and 200 MB for dedicated control planes.</p> <p>For more details, please refer to our <a href="/kubernetes/reference-content/kubernetes-control-plane-offers/">dedicated documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-size-limits-for-etcd-stores-in-kubernetes-control-planes https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-size-limits-for-etcd-stores-in-kubernetes-control-planes containers
<![CDATA[Generative-apis | Model library expanded]]> <p><a href="/managed-inference/reference-content/model-catalog/#llama-31-8b-instruct">Meta Llama 3.1 8b</a>, <a href="/managed-inference/reference-content/model-catalog/llama-31-70b-instruct">Meta Llama 3.1 70b</a> and <a href="/managed-inference/reference-content/model-catalog/#mistral-nemo-instruct-2407">Mistral Nemo</a> are available for deployment on Managed Inference.
</p> <p>Released July 2024, these models all support a very large context window of up to 128k tokens, particularly useful for RAG applications.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#model-library-expanded https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#model-library-expanded ai-data <![CDATA[Ipam | IPAM is now in General Availability]]> <p><a href="/ipam/quickstart/">IP Address Manager (IPAM)</a> offers several different functionalities in the Scaleway console such as viewing, tagging and filtering private IP addresses.
Users can also reserve a private IP on one of their Private Networks and use it to attach an Instance, Load Balancer or Public Gateway to the network.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#ipam-is-now-in-general-availability https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#ipam-is-now-in-general-availability network <![CDATA[Public-gateways | Public Gateway upgrade]]> <p>Users can now upgrade their Public Gateway to a higher range from the <a href="https://console.scaleway.com/">Scaleway console</a> or through the <a
href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/developers/api/public-gateways/">Public Gateway API</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#public-gateway-upgrade https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#public-gateway-upgrade network <![CDATA[Redistm | New Redis™ metrics in Cockpit]]> <p>New Redis metrics are available in Cockpit:</p> <ul> <li><strong>Network I/O Monitoring</strong>: Gain insights into network traffic and usage.</li> <li><strong>Key Hit/Miss Ratio</strong>: Track the ratio of cache hits to misses for improved cache performance.</li> <li><strong>Client Metrics</strong>:<ul>
<li>Monitor the number of active clients.</li> <li>Track blocked clients (e.g., during BLPOP operations).</li> </ul> </li> <li><strong>Key Expiration Stats</strong>:<ul> <li>Ratio of keys approaching expiration.</li> <li>Ratio of keys that have expired.</li> </ul> </li> <li><strong>Key Eviction Ratio</strong>: Monitor the ratio of keys evicted due to memory pressure.</li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-redistm-metrics-in-cockpit https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-redistm-metrics-in-cockpit databases <![CDATA[Vpc | VPC custom routes are now in General
Availability]]> <p>VPC routing now supports custom routes, for users who want to redirect part of the private traffic to a remote subnet through a VPN for example.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#vpc-custom-routes-are-now-in-general-availability https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#vpc-custom-routes-are-now-in-general-availability network <![CDATA[Object-storage | Lifecycle rules now allow to abort multipart uploads in the Scaleway console]]> <p>It is now possible to configure a lifecycle rule from the Scaleway console to automatically abort your incomplete multipart
uploads after a chosen number of days.
Learn <a href="/object-storage/how-to/manage-lifecycle-rules/">how to start configuring lifecycle rules</a> from the console.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#lifecycle-rules-now-allow-to-abort-multipart-uploads-in-the-scaleway-console https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#lifecycle-rules-now-allow-to-abort-multipart-uploads-in-the-scaleway-console storage <![CDATA[Object-storage | Incomplete multipart uploads are now visible in the Scaleway console!]]> <p>It is now possible to list and abort incomplete multipart uploads from the Scaleway console.
Follow the <a href="/object-storage/how-to/abort-incomplete-mpu">dedicated documentation</a> to navigate to your incomplete multipart uploads.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#incomplete-multipart-uploads-are-now-visible-in-the-scaleway-console https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#incomplete-multipart-uploads-are-now-visible-in-the-scaleway-console storage <![CDATA[Generative-apis | JSON mode and structured outputs]]> <p>All AI models can now reliably generate JSON output when required.
The <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/managed-inference/reference-content/openai-compatibility/#supported-parameters">Chat Completions API</a> supports the <code>response_format</code> parameter, which can be set to either <code>json_object</code> or <code>json_schema</code>, following OpenAI&#39;s specifications exactly.
</p> <p>Refer to <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/generative-apis/how-to/use-structured-outputs/">our documentation for code examples</a>, including usage with Pydantic models or manual schema definitions.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#json-mode-and-structured-outputs https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#json-mode-and-structured-outputs ai-data <![CDATA[Containers | Ephemeral storage is now customizable]]> <p>Previously, ephemeral storage for Serverless Containers was set to 1 GB.
Now, you can customize the ephemeral storage size at no additional cost.</p> <p>The minimum size is 1 GB, and the maximum size depends on your RAM, to an upper limit of 24 GB.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#ephemeral-storage-is-now-customizable https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#ephemeral-storage-is-now-customizable serverless <![CDATA[Containers | Relationship between vCPU and RAM rationalized]]> <p>Serverless Containers now imposes constraints on RAM based on the selected vCPU.
This is in order to promote coherent usage of memory and power.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#relationship-between-vcpu-and-ram-rationalized https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#relationship-between-vcpu-and-ram-rationalized serverless <![CDATA[Containers | Serverless Containers default sandbox set to v2]]> <p>To reduce cold-starts, we recently added a new version of our sandbox: v2.</p> <p>This is now the default sandbox version for new Functions on the console, as it offers enhanced performance.</p>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-containers-default-sandbox-set-to-v2 https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-containers-default-sandbox-set-to-v2 serverless <![CDATA[Functions | Serverless Functions default sandbox set to v2]]> <p>To reduce cold-starts, we recently added a new version of our sandbox: v2.</p> <p>This is now the default sandbox version for new Functions on the console, as it offers enhanced performance.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-functions-default-sandbox-set-to-v2
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#serverless-functions-default-sandbox-set-to-v2 serverless <![CDATA[Jobs | Creation and starting dates]]> <p>In the Scaleway console the <code>created_at</code> date appeared instead of the <code>started_at</code> date for some Serverless Jobs runs.
The bug is now fixed.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#creation-and-starting-dates https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#creation-and-starting-dates serverless <![CDATA[Generative-apis | Analyze images with Mistral vision language model]]> <p>You can now deploy your own secure <code>Pixtral-12b-2409</code> model with Scaleway Managed Inference.
</p> <p>With a privacy-focused, fully managed stack, this solution opens up new possibilities for applications that require both textual and visual understanding, knowing your images remain safe.</p> <p>Read <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/managed-inference/reference-content/pixtral-12b-2409">our dedicated documentation</a> to get started with Pixtral.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#analyze-images-with-mistral-vision-language-model https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#analyze-images-with-mistral-vision-language-model ai-data <![CDATA[Transactional-email |
Webhooks GA release - track and react to email events instantly]]> <p>Receive instant notifications on your email status (queued, delivered, dropped ...) and trigger automated actions in your integration.
Simply set up your endpoint URLs and choose the events you want to track for quick and seamless reactions!
Refer to the <a href="/transactional-email/how-to/create-webhooks/">How to create webhooks</a> documentation page for more information.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#webhooks-ga-release-track-and-react-to-email-events-instantly https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#webhooks-ga-release-track-and-react-to-email-events-instantly managed-services <![CDATA[Elastic-metal | New public bandwidth increasing option!]]> <p>On compliant Elastic Metal offers, you can now increase the <strong>public bandwidth</strong> as you wish through the console or API.
The eligible offers where you can increase the public bandwidth up to 10 Gbps are:</p> <ul> <li>EM-B220E-NVME</li> <li>EM-B320E-NVME</li> <li>EM-B420E-NVME</li> <li>EM-B520E-NVME</li> <li>EM-I115E-NVME</li> <li>EM-L220E-NVME</li> <li>EM-L420E-NVME</li> </ul> <p>To activate it, go to the <a href="https://console.scaleway.com/elastic-metal/servers">Elastic Metal server section</a> of the Scaleway console and click <strong>Settings</strong> &gt; <strong>Public bandwidth</strong> &gt; <strong>Manage bandwidth</strong>.</p> <p>Prices of this option are available <a
href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/pricing/elastic-metal/#network">here</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-public-bandwidth-increasing-option https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-public-bandwidth-increasing-option bare-metal <![CDATA[Object-storage | All requests now go through Scaleway Object Storage’s new gateway for all regions]]> <p>The rollout of the new gateway is now complete in all regions.
HTTP <code>PUT</code> requests now also go through the new gateway, leading to a significant performance improvement, especially on p50 and p90 percentiles.
The next updates will bring further improvements to p95, p99 and p99.9 percentiles for the <code>PUT</code> method.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#all-requests-now-go-through-scaleway-object-storages-new-gateway-for-all-regions https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#all-requests-now-go-through-scaleway-object-storages-new-gateway-for-all-regions storage <![CDATA[Object-storage | Increase of Object Storage default rate limiting in all Regions]]> <p>The default rate limiting threshold has been significantly increased on all requests for all Regions to better support
intensive workloads.
</p> <p>For users who still reach the limit (429 error code), the process for asking for a rate limit upgrade remains unchanged, through <a href="/account/how-to/open-a-support-ticket/">opening a support ticket</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#increase-of-object-storage-default-rate-limiting-in-all-regions https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#increase-of-object-storage-default-rate-limiting-in-all-regions storage <![CDATA[Billing | Discover our new Compute Savings Plan offer]]> <p>Scaleway’s Savings Plan is a commitment-based discount program that provides more
cost-effective billing compared to the pay-as-you-go model.
Currently, this offer can be used with <a href="/billing/additional-content/understanding-savings-plans/#compute-savings-plan">Instance products.</a> Access Savings Plans in the <a href="https://console.scaleway.com">Scaleway console</a> in the <strong>Cost optimization</strong> tab of your Billing space.</p> <p>Read <a href="/billing/additional-content/understanding-savings-plans/">our dedicated documentation</a> for more information and to purchase your first Savings Plan.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#discover-our-new-compute-savings-plan-offer
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#discover-our-new-compute-savings-plan-offer billing <![CDATA[Generative-apis | New public endpoints]]> <p>All new public endpoints for Managed Inference deployments will be using the <code>scaleway.com</code> domain.

https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-authentication-method-to-the-scaleway-console console <![CDATA[Kubernetes | New Kubernetes release and patch versions]]> <p>The following Kubernetes versions are now available along with new patch versions for earlier releases:</p> <ul> <li><code>1.33.0 – 1.33.4</code></li> <li><code>1.32.8</code></li> <li><code>1.31.12</code></li> </ul> <h3>Kubernetes 1.30 updates</h3> <ul> <li>Calico: <del>v3.28.x</del> → <code>v3.28.5</code></li> <li>Cilium: <del>v1.15.x</del> → <code>v1.15.19</code></li> <li>Metrics Server: <del>v0.7.x</del> →
