https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-kubernetes-release-and-patch-versions containers <![CDATA[Audit-trail | Elastic Metal events available in Audit Trail]]> <p>Elastic Metal activity can now be tracked in Audit Trail.
Read <a href="/audit-trail/reference-content/adt-supported-endpoints/">our documentation</a> to see the list of events available.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#elastic-metal-events-available-in-audit-trail https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#elastic-metal-events-available-in-audit-trail monitoring <![CDATA[Generative-apis | New SXM node types available]]> <p><strong>H100-SXM-2</strong>, <strong>H100-SXM-4</strong> and <strong>H100-SXM-8</strong> nodes are now available in Managed Inference.</p> <p><a href="https://console.scaleway.com/inference/deployments/fr-par">Try
them now</a> to benefit from improved token generation speed (up to x2 on most models).</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-sxm-node-types-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-sxm-node-types-available ai-data <![CDATA[Containers | JSON editor for environment variables and secrets]]> <p>In the Scaleway console, you can now create and edit environment variables using two modes: <strong>Classic</strong> and <strong>JSON</strong>.</p> <p>This makes it easier to import and manage multiple entries at once.</p>
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#json-editor-for-environment-variables-and-secrets https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#json-editor-for-environment-variables-and-secrets serverless <![CDATA[Apple-silicon | macOS 26 now available]]> <p>macOS Tahoe 26 is now available for installation on Mac minis M2 and M4.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#macos-26-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#macos-26-now-available bare-metal <![CDATA[Elastic-metal | Bulk order and Audit Trail integration]]> <p>Two new features for Elastic Metal are now
available:</p> <ul> <li>Bulk order: You can now place multiple orders for the same Elastic Metal resource in a single API request.</li> <li>Audit Trail: Elastic Metal activity can now be tracked in the Audit Trail product, directly in the console.
All API gateway events are listed.
Find out which endpoints are supported in the <a href="/audit-trail/reference-content/adt-supported-endpoints/">dedicated documentation</a>.</li> </ul> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#bulk-order-and-audit-trail-integration https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#bulk-order-and-audit-trail-integration bare-metal <![CDATA[Generative-apis | Voxtral small is now available]]> <p><a href="/generative-apis/reference-content/supported-models/">Voxtral Small 2507</a> is now available on Generative APIs.</p> <p>Voxtral is a frontier chat and audio model that can transcribe or understand
audio files using <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/developers/api/generative-apis/chat-completions">Chat Completions API</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#voxtral-small-is-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#voxtral-small-is-now-available ai-data <![CDATA[Iam | Organization aliases and dedicated URL for member login]]> <p>IAM admins can now set up an Organization alias within their Organization.
With this alias, a dedicated URL can be built for any member of the Organization to easily log in.
<a href="/organizations-and-projects/how-to/set-organization-alias/">Read our documentation</a> for more information on the feature.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#organization-aliases-and-dedicated-url-for-member-login https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#organization-aliases-and-dedicated-url-for-member-login security-identity <![CDATA[Audit-trail | Load Balancers events available in Audit Trail]]> <p>Load Balancers activity can now be tracked in Audit Trail.
Read <a href="/audit-trail/reference-content/adt-supported-endpoints/#load-balancers">our documentation</a> to see the list of events available.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#load-balancers-events-available-in-audit-trail https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#load-balancers-events-available-in-audit-trail monitoring <![CDATA[Domains-and-dns | New TLD available]]> <p>Scaleway now supports <code>.ai</code> domain name creation, renewal, and transfer.
This addition provides a strategic domain extension for businesses and organizations, especially in the artificial intelligence sector.
<code>.ai</code> domains require a minimum registration period of two years.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-tld-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-tld-available domains-and-webhosting <![CDATA[Environmental-footprint | Access raw data on your environmental impact via the API!]]> <p>We have released a new API that gives you programmatic, fine-grained access to your usage data.
You can now retrieve detailed environmental impact metrics, such as carbon emissions (kgCO₂e) and water consumption (m³), in raw JSON format.</p> <p>Get started and <a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/developers/api/environmental-footprint/user-api/">read the documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#access-raw-data-on-your-environmental-impact-via-the-api https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#access-raw-data-on-your-environmental-impact-via-the-api billing <![CDATA[Iam | Authentication logs for Members in Audit Trail]]> <p>Member login attempts and successes
are now tracked in Audit Trail.
Events for password, authentication code, OAuth2 and SAML logins now appear in the console and in the Audit Trail API.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#authentication-logs-for-members-in-audit-trail https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#authentication-logs-for-members-in-audit-trail security-identity <![CDATA[Iam | SAML authentication for Members in Early Access]]> <p>The SAML protocol is now supported for member authentication in Scaleway Organizations.
Once set up by an IAM administrator, the SAML connection becomes effective, and all members can use their Identity Provider sign-in to login to Scaleway.
<a href="/iam/how-to/set-up-identity-federation">Read our documentation</a> for more information on the feature, currently in Early Access phase.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#saml-authentication-for-members-in-early-access https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#saml-authentication-for-members-in-early-access security-identity <![CDATA[Kubernetes | New Kubernetes release and new CNI available]]> <h3>Kubernetes 1.34 release</h3> <ul> <li>Calico: <code>v3.30.3</code></li> <li>Cilium: <code>v1.18.1</code></li> <li>Metrics Server: <code>v0.8.0</code></li> <li>CoreDNS:
<code>v1.12.3</code></li> <li>CSI: <code>v0.3.5</code></li> <li>CCM: <code>v0.34.0</code></li> </ul> <p><strong>Node components:</strong></p> <ul> <li>Kernel: <code>6.8.0-64-generic</code></li> <li>Containerd: <code>2.1.4</code></li> <li>CNI: <code>1.7.1</code></li> <li>Runc: <code>1.3.1</code></li> </ul> <p><strong>Available Feature Gates in 1.34:</strong></p> <ul> <li><code>HPAScaleToZero</code></li> <li><code>CPUManagerPolicyAlphaOptions</code></li> <li><code>ImageVolume</code></li> </ul> <h3>New cilium_native CNI</h3> <p>On Kapsule 1.34, <code>cilium_native</code> is available as a new
CNI.
This is <code>cilium</code> with a custom configuration that enables its <code>native</code> mode.
For more information, please read <a href="https://docs.cilium.io/en/stable/network/concepts/routing/#native-routing">this cilium documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-kubernetes-release-and-new-cni-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-kubernetes-release-and-new-cni-available containers <![CDATA[Kubernetes | Kubernetes 1.37 released]]> <p>This release upgrades the Kubernetes control plane and node components to version 1.37, including updated networking, storage, and observability components.</p> <h3>Control plane &amp; add-ons</h3> <ul>
<li>Calico: <code>v3.32.1</code></li> <li>Cilium: <code>v1.20.1</code></li> <li>Kilo: <code>0.7.0</code></li> <li>Metrics Server: <code>v0.9.0</code></li> <li>CoreDNS: <code>v1.14.7</code></li> <li>Scaleway Block Storage CSI driver: <code>v0.3.6</code></li> <li>Scaleway File Storage CSI driver: <code>v0.0.3</code></li> <li>Cloud Controller Manager (CCM): <code>v0.36.2</code></li> </ul> <h3>Node changelog components</h3> <ul> <li>Linux kernel: <code>6.8.0-136-generic</code></li> <li>containerd: <code>2.3.4</code></li> <li>CNI plugins: <code>1.9.1</code></li> <li>runc: <code>1.5.1</code></li>
</ul> <h3>Feature gates available in 1.37</h3> <p>The following feature gates are available and can be enabled as needed:</p> <ul> <li><code>HPAScaleToZero</code></li> <li><code>CPUManagerPolicyAlphaOptions</code></li> <li><code>MutatingAdmissionPolicy</code></li> <li><code>ImageVolume</code></li> </ul> <p>For more information, refer to <a href="/kubernetes/reference-content/version-support-policy/">Scaleway&#39;s Kubernetes version support policy</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#kubernetes-137-released https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#kubernetes-137-released
containers <![CDATA[Iam | Scaleway-managed IAM groups - All Users and All Applications]]> <p>Two new Scaleway-managed IAM groups have been added in all Organizations: </p> <p>The <strong>All Users</strong> group dynamically contains all the users in your Organization The <strong>All Applications</strong> group dynamically contains all the applications in your Organization, except the Scaleway-managed applications.</p> <p>You can use these two groups to manage permissions at scale without the need to manually manage group members.</p>

We recommend you update to Python 3.7.</li> </ul> <p>For more information please refer to our <a href="/serverless-functions/reference-content/functions-runtimes/">dedicated documentation</a>.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#python-runtimes-will-reach-the-end-of-support-state https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#python-runtimes-will-reach-the-end-of-support-state serverless <![CDATA[Object-storage | New Object Storage permission sets are available]]> <p>Identity and Access Management (IAM) is now available to manage users and applications&#39; access to objects and
buckets.
In each Scaleway Project, you can now give permissions to create, read, edit and/or delete object and buckets.</p> <p>You can find a list of the new Object Storage permission sets in the <a href="/iam/reference-content/permission-sets/">IAM permission sets</a> documentation page.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-object-storage-permission-sets-are-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#new-object-storage-permission-sets-are-available storage <![CDATA[Object-storage | The integration of Object Storage metrics in our Observability solution is now available!]]>
<p>You can now monitor your buckets&#39; usage metrics with Scaleway&#39;s Observability solution.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#the-integration-of-object-storage-metrics-in-our-observability-solution-is-now-available https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#the-integration-of-object-storage-metrics-in-our-observability-solution-is-now-available storage <![CDATA[Object-storage | HTTP/2 is now supported on all endpoints.]]> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#http2-is-now-supported-on-all-endpoints
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#http2-is-now-supported-on-all-endpoints storage <![CDATA[Object-storage | Our new object storage backend has been deployed on all regions]]> <p>Object Storage now supports the highly redundant MultiAZ infrastructure for its Standard class in the Paris region.
Built on a Scaleway-designed stack, it takes data redundancy, resiliency &amp; scalability to the next level.</p> <p>A new storage class is also available in the Paris region: One Zone &#8211; IA (Infrequent Access).</p> <p>This storage class is a good choice for storing secondary backup copies or easily recreate data.
It can also be integrated into your lifecycle management rules, along with Standard and Glacier classes.
</p> <ul> <li><a href="https://www.scaleway.com/en/blog/improve-disaster-resiliency-with-highly-redundant-multi-az-object-storage/">Improve disaster resiliency with highly redundant Multi-AZ Object Storage</a></li> <li><a href="/object-storage/faq/">New Storage Classes - FAQ</a></li> </ul> <p>On Buckets created starting January the 27th, you will be able to store billions of objects inside a single bucket without any performance issues.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#our-new-object-storage-backend-has-been-deployed-on-all-regions
https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#our-new-object-storage-backend-has-been-deployed-on-all-regions storage <![CDATA[Object-storage | Upgraded to a new version of our (meta)database core engine]]> <p>New version of our (meta)database core engine (aka “Hive”), that includes improvements and bug fixes.</p> https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#upgraded-to-a-new-version-of-our-metadatabase-core-engine https://www.scaleway.com/en/docs/changelog/#upgraded-to-a-new-version-of-our-metadatabase-core-engine storage
